}


def msg(key: str, *args) -> str:
    text = MESSAGES_AR.get(key, key)
    if args:
        text = text.format(*args)
    return text
''')


# ----------------------------
# arabic utils
# ----------------------------
add("src/core/arabic_utils.py", r'''import re
import unicodedata


ARABIC_DIACRITICS = re.compile(r"[\u064B-\u0652\u0670\u06D6-\u06ED\u0640]")
NON_WORD = re.compile(r"[^\u0600-\u06FFa-z0-9\s]", re.IGNORECASE)
SPACES = re.compile(r"\s+")

NORMALIZE_MAP = str.maketrans({
    "أ": "ا",
    "إ": "ا",
    "آ": "ا",
    "ٱ": "ا",
    "ة": "ه",
    "ى": "ي",
    "ؤ": "و",
    "ئ": "ي",
})

STOPWORDS_AR = {"ماده", "تخصص", "اختياري", "اختصاص", "متقدم", "عامه", "اساسيه"}
STOPWORDS_EN = {"language", "optional", "for", "subject", "advanced"}


def normalize_arabic(text: str) -> str:
    if text is None:
        return ""

    text = str(text).strip()
    if not text:
        return ""

    text = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", text).lower()
    text = ARABIC_DIACRITICS.sub("", text)
    text = text.translate(NORMALIZE_MAP)
    text = NON_WORD.sub(" ", text)
    text = SPACES.sub(" ", text).strip()

    words = []
    for w in text.split():
        if w in STOPWORDS_EN or w in STOPWORDS_AR:
            continue
        if len(w) > 3 and w.startswith("ال") and re.match(r"^[\u0600-\u06FF]", w):
            w = w[2:]
        words.append(w)

    return " ".join(words).strip()
''')


# ----------------------------
# phone utils
# ----------------------------
add("src/core/phone_utils.py", r'''import re
from src.core.constants import PHONE_REGEX, PHONE_PREFIXES


def normalize_jordan_mobile(value: str) -> str:
    if value is None:
        return ""

    digits = re.sub(r"\D", "", str(value))

    if digits.startswith("962") and len(digits) >= 12:
        digits = "0" + digits[3:]

    if len(digits) == 9 and re.match(r"^7[6789]", digits):
        digits = "0" + digits

    return digits


def is_valid_jordan_mobile(value: str) -> bool:
    normalized = normalize_jordan_mobile(value)
    return bool(re.match(PHONE_REGEX, normalized))


def validate_phone_field(value: str):
    normalized = normalize_jordan_mobile(value)

    if not normalized:
        return False, "رقم الهاتف مطلوب."

    if len(normalized) != 10:
        return False, "رقم الهاتف يجب أن يتكون من 10 خانات."

    if not normalized.startswith(PHONE_PREFIXES):
        return False, "رقم الهاتف يجب أن يبدأ بـ 076 أو 077 أو 078 أو 079."

    if not re.match(PHONE_REGEX, normalized):
        return False, "رقم الهاتف غير صالح."

    return True, normalized
''')


# ----------------------------
# validators
# ----------------------------
add("src/core/validators.py", r'''import re
from src.core.constants import MINISTERIAL_LENGTH, NATIONAL_LENGTH
from src.core.phone_utils import validate_phone_field


def detect_login_type(value: str):
    value = value.strip()
    if not value.isdigit():
        return None
    if len(value) == MINISTERIAL_LENGTH:
        return "MINISTERIAL"
    if len(value) == NATIONAL_LENGTH:
        return "NATIONAL"
    return None


def validate_national_id(value: str) -> bool:
    return bool(re.match(r"^\d{10}$", value.strip()))


def validate_ministerial_number(value: str) -> bool:
    return bool(re.match(r"^\d{6}$", value.strip()))


def validate_required(text: str, label: str):
    if not text or not text.strip():
        return False, f"{label} مطلوب."
    return True, text.strip()


def validate_before_submit(data: dict):
    errors = []

    if not data.get("school_id"):
        errors.append("يجب تحديد المدرسة.")

    if not data.get("employee_id"):
        errors.append("يجب اختيار موظف صالح.")

    ok, phone_msg = validate_phone_field(data.get("mobile_phone", ""))
    if not ok:
        errors.append(phone_msg)

    if not data.get("acknowledgement"):
        errors.append("يجب الإقرار بمسؤولية دقة البيانات.")

    return errors
''')


# ----------------------------
# app context
# ----------------------------
add("src/core/app_context.py", r'''from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Optional


@dataclass
class AppContext:
    current_user_id: Optional[int] = None
    current_user_name: str = ""
    current_role: str = ""

    selected_school_id: Optional[int] = None
    selected_school_name: str = ""

    selected_employee_id: Optional[int] = None
    selected_employee_name: str = ""

    active_form_id: Optional[int] = None
    active_form_code: str = ""

    failed_login_attempts: int = 0
    max_login_attempts: int = 3

    acknowledgement_given: bool = False

    drafts: dict = field(default_factory=dict)

    def require_school(self) -> bool:
        return self.selected_school_id is not None

    def require_employee(self) -> bool:
        return self.selected_employee_id is not None

    def reset_employee(self):
        self.selected_employee_id = None
        self.selected_employee_name = ""
''')


# ----------------------------
# db connection
# ----------------------------
add("src/db/connection.py", r'''import sqlite3
import sys
from pathlib import Path


if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
    BASE_DIR = Path(sys.executable).parent
else:
    BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DB_PATH = BASE_DIR / "exam2026.db"


def get_connection(db_path: Path = DB_PATH) -> sqlite3.Connection:
    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path))
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON")
    conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL")
    return conn
''')


# ----------------------------
# schema

add("src/db/schema.sql", r'''PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS settings (
    setting_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    setting_value TEXT,
    description TEXT,
    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS roles (
    role_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    role_code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    role_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    is_manual INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    created_by INTEGER,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS permissions (
    permission_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    permission_code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    permission_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    module TEXT,
    is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    is_manual INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
    created_by INTEGER,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    approved_by INTEGER,
    approved_at TEXT
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS role_permissions (
    role_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    permission_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    granted_by INTEGER,
    granted_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    PRIMARY KEY (role_id, permission_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (role_id) REFERENCES roles(role_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (permission_id) REFERENCES permissions(permission_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
    user_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    full_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    national_id TEXT UNIQUE,
    ministerial_number TEXT UNIQUE,
    role_id INTEGER,
    password_hash TEXT,
    is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    failed_login_attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    locked_until TEXT,
    last_login_at TEXT,
    created_manually INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    manual_reason TEXT,
    requested_by INTEGER,
    approved_by INTEGER,
    approval_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'APPROVED',
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TEXT,
    FOREIGN KEY (role_id) REFERENCES roles(role_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS login_attempts (
    attempt_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    login_value TEXT,
    detected_type TEXT,
    success INTEGER NOT NULL,
    message TEXT,
    hostname TEXT,
    machine_user TEXT,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS audit_logs (
    audit_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    table_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    record_id INTEGER,
    action TEXT NOT NULL,
    old_values TEXT,
    new_values TEXT,
    user_id INTEGER,
    hostname TEXT,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS directorates (
    directorate_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    directorate_name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schools (
    school_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    school_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    national_code TEXT,
    directorate_id INTEGER,
    branch_name TEXT,
    section_name TEXT,
    phone TEXT,
    address TEXT,
    is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    FOREIGN KEY (directorate_id) REFERENCES directorates(directorate_id)
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_schools_national_code
ON schools(national_code) WHERE national_code IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_schools_name ON schools(school_name);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS subjects (
    subject_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    canonical_name_pdf TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    name_en TEXT,
    category TEXT,
    grade_levels TEXT,
    normalized_key TEXT NOT NULL,
    is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    source TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PDF',
    is_pdf_canonical INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    raw_excel_value TEXT,
    needs_review INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_subjects_source ON subjects(source);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_subjects_status ON subjects(status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_subjects_is_pdf_canonical ON subjects(is_pdf_canonical);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS subject_aliases (
    alias_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    subject_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    alias_text TEXT NOT NULL,
    normalized_alias TEXT NOT NULL,
    source TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'manual',
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    FOREIGN KEY (subject_id) REFERENCES subjects(subject_id),
    UNIQUE(normalized_alias)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS employees (
    employee_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    national_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    ministerial_number TEXT UNIQUE,
    full_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    gender TEXT,
    job_title TEXT,
    directorate_id INTEGER,
    school_id INTEGER,
    specialization_id INTEGER,
    years_experience INTEGER,
    grade TEXT,
    residence TEXT,
    mobile_phone TEXT NOT NULL,
    university_name TEXT,
    is_new_employee INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    notes TEXT,
    excel_source_file TEXT,
    excel_row_number INTEGER,
    last_auto_filled_at TEXT,
    created_by INTEGER,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TEXT,
    FOREIGN KEY (specialization_id) REFERENCES subjects(subject_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (school_id) REFERENCES schools(school_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (directorate_id) REFERENCES directorates(directorate_id)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_employees_national ON employees(national_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_employees_ministerial ON employees(ministerial_number);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS excel_employee_import (
    import_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    source_file TEXT NOT NULL,
    sheet_name TEXT,
    row_number INTEGER,
    national_id TEXT,
    ministerial_number TEXT,
    full_name TEXT,
    school_name TEXT,
    school_national_code TEXT,
    directorate_name TEXT,
    specialization_raw TEXT,
    resolved_specialization_id INTEGER,
    specialization_source TEXT,
    mobile_phone_raw TEXT,
    normalized_mobile_phone TEXT,
    years_experience INTEGER,
    grade TEXT,
    residence TEXT,
    university_name TEXT,
    is_new_employee INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    notes TEXT,
    raw_json TEXT NOT NULL,
    imported_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    FOREIGN KEY (resolved_specialization_id) REFERENCES subjects(subject_id)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_excel_import_national ON excel_employee_import(national_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_excel_import_ministerial ON excel_employee_import(ministerial_number);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS forms (
    form_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    form_code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    form_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    revision TEXT,
    effective_year INTEGER,
    is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS exam_cycles (
    cycle_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    cycle_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    year INTEGER NOT NULL,
    exam_type TEXT,
    start_date TEXT,
    end_date TEXT,
    is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS submissions (
    submission_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    form_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    employee_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    cycle_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    school_id INTEGER,
    directorate_id INTEGER,
    status INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    is_locked INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    locked_at TEXT,
    locked_by INTEGER,
    entered_by INTEGER NOT NULL,
    entered_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    approved_by INTEGER,
    approved_at TEXT,
    approval_note TEXT,
    rejection_reason TEXT,
    pdf_path TEXT,
    excel_path TEXT,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TEXT,
    FOREIGN KEY (form_id) REFERENCES forms(form_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (employee_id) REFERENCES employees(employee_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (cycle_id) REFERENCES exam_cycles(cycle_id),
    UNIQUE (employee_id, form_id, cycle_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS drafts (
    draft_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    form_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    employee_id INTEGER,
    school_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    cycle_id INTEGER,
    user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT',
    json_data TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    FOREIGN KEY (form_id) REFERENCES forms(form_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (employee_id) REFERENCES employees(employee_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (school_id) REFERENCES schools(school_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS form_acknowledgments (
    acknowledgment_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    school_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    employee_id INTEGER,
    form_id INTEGER,
    submission_id INTEGER,
    note_version TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '2026-v1',
    acknowledged_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    hostname TEXT,
    FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (school_id) REFERENCES schools(school_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS attachments (
    attachment_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    submission_id INTEGER,
    employee_id INTEGER,
    file_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    original_file_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_path TEXT NOT NULL,
    mime_type TEXT,
    file_size INTEGER,
    category TEXT NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    uploaded_by INTEGER NOT NULL,
    uploaded_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    FOREIGN KEY (submission_id) REFERENCES submissions(submission_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (employee_id) REFERENCES employees(employee_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS exports (
    export_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    submission_id INTEGER,
    report_name TEXT,
    export_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_path TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_hash TEXT,
    is_protected INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    protection_level TEXT,
    generated_by INTEGER NOT NULL,
    generated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    FOREIGN KEY (submission_id) REFERENCES submissions(submission_id)
);
''')


# ----------------------------
# login window
# ----------------------------
add("src/ui/login_window.py", r'''import socket
import getpass
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk, messagebox
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

from src.core.constants import (
    APP_TITLE, COPYRIGHT, MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS,
    MINISTERIAL_LENGTH, NATIONAL_LENGTH, ORG_NAME, DEPT_NAME
)
from src.core.validators import detect_login_type
from src.core.messages_ar import msg
from src.db.connection import get_connection


class LoginApp:
    def __init__(self):
        self.root = tk.Tk()
        self.attempts = 0
        self.conn = None
        self._build_ui()

    def run(self):
        self.root.mainloop()

    def _build_ui(self):
        self.root.title(APP_TITLE)
        self.root.resizable(False, False)

        sw = self.root.winfo_screenwidth()
        sh = self.root.winfo_screenheight()
        w = min(560, int(sw * 0.9))
        h = min(420, int(sh * 0.85))
        x = (sw - w) // 2
        y = (sh - h) // 2
        self.root.geometry(f"{w}x{h}+{x}+{y}")

        style = ttk.Style()
        style.configure("Title.TLabel", font=("Arial", 14, "bold"))
        style.configure("Sub.TLabel", font=("Arial", 10))
        style.configure("TButton", font=("Arial", 10))
        style.configure("TEntry", font=("Arial", 11))

        main = ttk.Frame(self.root, padding=20)
        main.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True)

        ttk.Label(
            main,
            text=ORG_NAME + "\n" + DEPT_NAME,
            style="Sub.TLabel",
            justify=tk.CENTER
        ).pack(pady=(0, 5))

        ttk.Label(
            main,
            text=APP_TITLE,
            style="Title.TLabel",
            justify=tk.CENTER
        ).pack(pady=(0, 20))

        form = ttk.Frame(main)
        form.pack(pady=10)

        ttk.Label(form, text="رقم الدخول:").grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="e", padx=5, pady=8)

        self.login_var = tk.StringVar()
        self.login_entry = ttk.Entry(form, textvariable=self.login_var, width=28, justify=tk.CENTER)
        self.login_entry.grid(row=0, column=1, padx=5, pady=8)
        self.login_entry.focus_set()

        ttk.Label(
            form,
            text="أدخل الرقم الوزاري (6 أرقام) أو الرقم الوطني (10 أرقام)",
            style="Sub.TLabel"
        ).grid(row=1, column=0, columnspan=2, pady=(0, 10))

        buttons = ttk.Frame(main)
        buttons.pack(pady=10)

        ttk.Button(buttons, text="دخول", command=self._attempt_login).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5)
        ttk.Button(buttons, text="خروج", command=self._confirm_exit).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5)

        ttk.Label(main, text=COPYRIGHT, style="Sub.TLabel").pack(side=tk.BOTTOM, pady=5)

        self.root.bind("<Return>", lambda e: self._attempt_login())
        self.root.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", self._confirm_exit)

    def _attempt_login(self):
        value = self.login_var.get().strip()
        login_type = detect_login_type(value)

        self.conn = get_connection()

        if not login_type:
            self.attempts += 1
            self._log_attempt(value, None, False, "مدخل غير صالح")

            if self.attempts >= MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS:
                messagebox.showerror("إنهاء الجلسة", msg("LOGIN_EXIT"))
                self.conn.close()
                self.root.destroy()
                return

            messagebox.showwarning(
                "محاولة غير صالحة",
                msg("LOGIN_INVALID_FORMAT") + "\n" + msg("LOGIN_ATTEMPTS_LEFT", MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS - self.attempts)
            )
            self.conn.close()
            return

        user_row, message = self._validate_user(value, login_type)

        if user_row:
            self._reset_attempts(user_row["user_id"])
            self._log_attempt(value, login_type, True, "نجاح")
            self.conn.close()
            messagebox.showinfo("نجاح", msg("LOGIN_SUCCESS") + "\n" + user_row["full_name"])
            self.root.destroy()
            self._open_main(user_row)
            return

        self.attempts += 1
        self._register_failed(value, login_type, message)
        self._log_attempt(value, login_type, False, message)

        if self.attempts >= MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS:
            messagebox.showerror("إنهاء الجلسة", msg("LOGIN_EXIT"))
            self.conn.close()
            self.root.destroy()
            return

        messagebox.showwarning(
            "فشل الدخول",
            message + "\n" + msg("LOGIN_ATTEMPTS_LEFT", MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS - self.attempts)
        )
        self.conn.close()

    def _validate_user(self, value, login_type):
        cur = self.conn.cursor()
        col = "ministerial_number" if login_type == "MINISTERIAL" else "national_id"
        cur.execute(f"""
            SELECT u.user_id,
                   u.full_name,
                   COALESCE(r.role_name, 'مستخدم') AS role,
                   u.is_active,
                   u.failed_login_attempts,
                   u.locked_until
            FROM users u
            LEFT JOIN roles r ON r.role_id = u.role_id
            WHERE u.{col} = ?
        """, (value,))
        row = cur.fetchone()

        if not row:
            return None, msg("LOGIN_FAILED")
        if not row["is_active"]:
            return None, "الحساب غير مفعّل."
        if row["locked_until"]:
            try:
                lu = datetime.fromisoformat(row["locked_until"])
                if datetime.now() < lu:
                    return None, "الحساب مقفل مؤقتًا."
            except Exception:
                pass
        return row, "OK"

    def _register_failed(self, value, login_type, message):
        cur = self.conn.cursor()
        col = "ministerial_number" if login_type == "MINISTERIAL" else "national_id"
        cur.execute(f"SELECT user_id, failed_login_attempts FROM users WHERE {col} = ?", (value,))
        row = cur.fetchone()
        if row:
            attempts = row["failed_login_attempts"] + 1
            locked = None
            if attempts >= MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS:
                locked = (datetime.now() + timedelta(minutes=15)).isoformat()
            cur.execute("""
                UPDATE users SET failed_login_attempts = ?, locked_until = ? WHERE user_id = ?
            """, (attempts, locked, row["user_id"]))
            self.conn.commit()

    def _reset_attempts(self, user_id):
        cur = self.conn.cursor()
        cur.execute("""
            UPDATE users SET failed_login_attempts = 0, locked_until = NULL, last_login_at = ?
            WHERE user_id = ?
        """, (datetime.now().isoformat(), user_id))
        self.conn.commit()

    def _log_attempt(self, value, ltype, success, message):
        cur = self.conn.cursor()
        cur.execute("""
            INSERT INTO login_attempts(login_value, detected_type, success, message, hostname, machine_user)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (value, ltype, 1 if success else 0, message, socket.gethostname(), getpass.getuser()))
        self.conn.commit()

    def _confirm_exit(self):
        if messagebox.askyesno("خروج", "هل تريد الخروج من النظام؟"):
            self.root.destroy()

    def _open_main(self, user_row):
        from src.ui.main_window import MainWindow
        from src.core.app_context import AppContext

        ctx = AppContext()
        ctx.current_user_id = user_row["user_id"]
        ctx.current_user_name = user_row["full_name"]
        ctx.current_role = user_row["role"]

        app = MainWindow(ctx)
        app.run()
''')

# main window

add("src/ui/main_window.py", r'''import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk, messagebox
import webbrowser

from src.core.constants import APP_TITLE, COPYRIGHT, ORG_NAME, DEPT_NAME
from src.core.messages_ar import msg
from src.core.app_context import AppContext


class MainWindow:
    def __init__(self, ctx: AppContext):
        self.ctx = ctx
        self.root = tk.Tk()
        self._build_ui()

    def run(self):
        self.root.mainloop()

    def _build_ui(self):
        self.root.title(APP_TITLE)

        sw = self.root.winfo_screenwidth()
        sh = self.root.winfo_screenheight()
        w = int(sw * 0.85)
        h = int(sh * 0.85)
        w = max(w, 980)
        h = max(h, 700)
        x = (sw - w) // 2
        y = (sh - h) // 2
        self.root.geometry(f"{w}x{h}+{x}+{y}")
        self.root.minsize(980, 700)

        menu = tk.Menu(self.root)

        file_menu = tk.Menu(menu, tearoff=0)
        file_menu.add_command(label="خروج", command=self._exit)
        menu.add_cascade(label="ملف", menu=file_menu)

        forms_menu = tk.Menu(menu, tearoff=0)
        forms_menu.add_command(label="المراقبة الإلكترونية", command=lambda: self._open_form("ELECTRONIC"))
        forms_menu.add_command(label="مراقبة المدراء QF71-4-6", command=lambda: self._open_form("QF71-4-6"))
        forms_menu.add_command(label="الجامعات QF71-4-9", command=lambda: self._open_form("QF71-4-9"))
        menu.add_cascade(label="النماذج", menu=forms_menu)

        admin_menu = tk.Menu(menu, tearoff=0)
        admin_menu.add_command(label="إدارة المستخدمين", command=self._coming_soon)
        admin_menu.add_command(label="إدارة الأذونات", command=self._coming_soon)
        admin_menu.add_command(label="إدارة المدارس", command=self._coming_soon)
        admin_menu.add_command(label="سجل التدقيق", command=self._coming_soon)
        menu.add_cascade(label="الإدارة", menu=admin_menu)

        help_menu = tk.Menu(menu, tearoff=0)
        help_menu.add_command(label="المساعدة والتواصل", command=self._open_help)
        help_menu.add_command(label="رابط التوثيق", command=self._open_doc_url)
        help_menu.add_command(label="رابط الذكاء الاصطناعي", command=self._open_ai_url)
        menu.add_cascade(label="المساعدة", menu=help_menu)

        self.root.config(menu=menu)

        header = ttk.Frame(self.root, padding=10)
        header.pack(fill=tk.X)

        ttk.Label(header, text=ORG_NAME + " — " + DEPT_NAME, font=("Arial", 11, "bold")).pack(side=tk.RIGHT)
        ttk.Label(header, text="المستخدم: " + self.ctx.current_user_name + " | الدور: " + self.ctx.current_role).pack(side=tk.LEFT)

        self.school_bar = ttk.Frame(self.root, padding=5)
        self.school_bar.pack(fill=tk.X)

        self.school_label = ttk.Label(self.school_bar, text="المدرسة: لم تُحدد بعد", font=("Arial", 10, "bold"))
        self.school_label.pack(side=tk.RIGHT)

        ttk.Button(self.school_bar, text="تحديد / تغيير المدرسة", command=self._select_school).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5)

        ack_frame = ttk.LabelFrame(self.root, text="تنبيه هام", padding=8)
        ack_frame.pack(fill=tk.X, padx=10, pady=5)

        ttk.Label(
            ack_frame,
            text="هذا العمل يتطلب أمانة ودقة في البيانات. مُدخل البيانات ومدير المدرسة يتحملان المسؤولية الكاملة.",
            wraplength=900,
            justify=tk.RIGHT,
            foreground="darkred"
        ).pack()

        from src.ui.employee_lookup_panel import EmployeeLookupPanel
        self.employee_panel = EmployeeLookupPanel(self.root, self.ctx)
        self.employee_panel.pack(fill=tk.X, padx=10, pady=5)

        self.content = ttk.Frame(self.root, padding=10)
        self.content.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True)

        ttk.Label(
            self.content,
            text="1) حدد المدرسة أولًا.\n2) أدخل رقم الموظف الوطني أو الوزاري لجلب البيانات تلقائيًا.\n3) ثم افتح النموذج المطلوب من قائمة النماذج.",
            font=("Arial", 12),
            justify=tk.RIGHT
        ).pack(expand=True)

        ttk.Label(self.root, text=COPYRIGHT, anchor=tk.CENTER, relief=tk.RIDGE, padding=4).pack(side=tk.BOTTOM, fill=tk.X)

    def _select_school(self):
        from src.ui.school_selection_window import SchoolSelectionWindow
        win = SchoolSelectionWindow(self.root, self.ctx)
        self.root.wait_window(win)
        if self.ctx.selected_school_id:
            self.school_label.config(text="المدرسة: " + self.ctx.selected_school_name)
            self.employee_panel.clear()
            messagebox.showinfo("تم", msg("SCHOOL_SELECTED", self.ctx.selected_school_name))

    def _open_form(self, form_code: str):
        if not self.ctx.require_school():
            messagebox.showwarning("تنبيه", msg("SCHOOL_REQUIRED"))
            self._select_school()
            if not self.ctx.require_school():
                return

        if not self.ctx.require_employee():
            messagebox.showwarning("تنبيه", "يجب إدخال رقم الموظف وجلب بياناته أولًا.")
            return

        from src.ui.acknowledgement_dialog import AcknowledgementDialog
        ack = AcknowledgementDialog(self.root, self.ctx)
        self.root.wait_window(ack)

        if not self.ctx.acknowledgement_given:
            messagebox.showwarning("تنبيه", msg("ACK_REQUIRED"))
            return

        messagebox.showinfo("النموذج", "تم استيفاء شروط فتح النموذج: " + form_code + "\nسيتم ربط شاشات النماذج الكاملة في المرحلة التالية.")

    def _coming_soon(self):
        messagebox.showinfo("قريبًا", "هذه الشاشة مخصصة للمرحلة التالية من التطوير.")

    def _open_help(self):
        from src.ui.help_window import HelpWindow
        HelpWindow(self.root)

    def _open_doc_url(self):
        from src.core.constants import DOC_URL
        webbrowser.open(DOC_URL)

    def _open_ai_url(self):
        from src.core.constants import AI_URL
        webbrowser.open(AI_URL)

    def _exit(self):
        if messagebox.askyesno("خروج", "هل تريد الخروج؟"):
            self.root.destroy()
''')


# ----------------------------
# school selection

add("src/ui/school_selection_window.py", r'''import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk, messagebox

from src.core.app_context import AppContext
from src.core.messages_ar import msg
from src.db.connection import get_connection


class SchoolSelectionWindow(tk.Toplevel):
    def __init__(self, parent, ctx: AppContext):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.ctx = ctx
        self.title("تحديد المدرسة")
        self.geometry("650x480")
        self.transient(parent)
        self.grab_set()
        self._rows = []
        self._build_ui()

    def _build_ui(self):
        main = ttk.Frame(self, padding=15)
        main.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True)

        ttk.Label(main, text="ابحث بالمدرسة (الاسم أو الرمز الوطني):", font=("Arial", 11, "bold")).pack(anchor="w")

        search_frame = ttk.Frame(main)
        search_frame.pack(fill=tk.X, pady=5)

        self.search_var = tk.StringVar()
        self.search_entry = ttk.Entry(search_frame, textvariable=self.search_var, width=45)
        self.search_entry.pack(side=tk.LEFT, fill=tk.X, expand=True)
        self.search_entry.bind("<KeyRelease>", lambda e: self._search())
        self.search_entry.bind("<Return>", lambda e: self._search())

        ttk.Button(search_frame, text="بحث", command=self._search).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5)

        self.results = tk.Listbox(main, height=14, font=("Arial", 10))
        self.results.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True, pady=5)

        btns = ttk.Frame(main)
        btns.pack(fill=tk.X, pady=5)

        ttk.Button(btns, text="اختيار", command=self._confirm).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5)
        ttk.Button(btns, text="إلغاء", command=self.destroy).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5)

        self._search()

    def _search(self):
        query = self.search_var.get().strip()
        conn = get_connection()
        cur = conn.cursor()

        if query:
            cur.execute("""
                SELECT school_id, school_name, national_code
                FROM schools
                WHERE is_active = 1
                  AND (
                      school_name LIKE ?
                      OR national_code LIKE ?
                  )
                ORDER BY school_name
                LIMIT 100
            """, (f"%{query}%", f"%{query}%"))
        else:
            cur.execute("""
                SELECT school_id, school_name, national_code
                FROM schools
                WHERE is_active = 1
                ORDER BY school_name
                LIMIT 100
            """)

        self.results.delete(0, tk.END)
        self._rows = cur.fetchall()

        for row in self._rows:
            label = row["school_name"]
            if row["national_code"]:
                label += "  [" + row["national_code"] + "]"
            self.results.insert(tk.END, label)

        if not self._rows:
            self.results.insert(tk.END, "— لا توجد نتائج —")

        conn.close()

    def _confirm(self):
        sel = self.results.curselection()
        if not sel:
            messagebox.showwarning("تنبيه", "اختر مدرسة من القائمة.")
            return

        idx = sel[0]
        if idx >= len(self._rows):
            return

        row = self._rows[idx]
        self.ctx.selected_school_id = row["school_id"]
        self.ctx.selected_school_name = row["school_name"]
        self.destroy()
''')


# ----------------------------
# employee lookup panel
# ----------------------------
add("src/ui/employee_lookup_panel.py", r'''import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk, messagebox

from src.core.app_context import AppContext
from src.core.validators import detect_login_type
from src.core.messages_ar import msg
from src.db.connection import get_connection


class EmployeeLookupPanel(ttk.LabelFrame):
    def __init__(self, parent, ctx: AppContext):
        super().__init__(parent, text="جلب بيانات الموظف تلقائيًا", padding=10)
        self.ctx = ctx

        row1 = ttk.Frame(self)
        row1.pack(fill=tk.X)

        ttk.Label(row1, text="رقم الموظف الوطني أو الوزاري:").pack(side=tk.RIGHT)

        self.number_var = tk.StringVar()
        self.entry = ttk.Entry(row1, textvariable=self.number_var, width=25, justify=tk.CENTER)
        self.entry.pack(side=tk.RIGHT, padx=10)
        self.entry.bind("<Return>", lambda e: self.search())

        ttk.Button(row1, text="بحث تلقائي", command=self.search).pack(side=tk.RIGHT, padx=5)

        self.info_label = ttk.Label(self, text="لم يتم اختيار موظف بعد.", foreground="gray")
        self.info_label.pack(anchor="w", pady=(8, 0))

    def clear(self):
        self.number_var.set("")
        self.ctx.reset_employee()
        self.info_label.config(text="لم يتم اختيار موظف بعد.", foreground="gray")

    def search(self):
        if not self.ctx.require_school():
            messagebox.showwarning("تنبيه", msg("SCHOOL_REQUIRED"))
            return

        value = self.number_var.get().strip()
        login_type = detect_login_type(value)

        if not login_type:
            messagebox.showwarning("تنبيه", msg("EMPLOYEE_NUMBER_INVALID"))
            return

        conn = get_connection()
        cur = conn.cursor()
        col = "ministerial_number" if login_type == "MINISTERIAL" else "national_id"

        cur.execute(f"""
            SELECT e.employee_id,
                   e.full_name,
                   e.national_id,
                   e.ministerial_number,
                   e.mobile_phone,
                   s.school_name,
                   sub.canonical_name_pdf AS specialization,
                   sub.source AS specialization_source
            FROM employees e
            LEFT JOIN schools s ON s.school_id = e.school_id
            LEFT JOIN subjects sub ON sub.subject_id = e.specialization_id
            WHERE e.{col} = ?
              AND e.school_id = ?
        """, (value, self.ctx.selected_school_id))

        row = cur.fetchone()
        conn.close()

        if not row:
            self.ctx.reset_employee()
            self.info_label.config(text=msg("EMPLOYEE_NOT_FOUND"), foreground="darkred")
            messagebox.showwarning("تنبيه", msg("EMPLOYEE_NOT_FOUND"))
            return

        self.ctx.selected_employee_id = row["employee_id"]
        self.ctx.selected_employee_name = row["full_name"]

        spec = row["specialization"] or "غير محدد"
        source = row["specialization_source"] or "-"
        phone = row["mobile_phone"] or "-"
        school = row["school_name"] or self.ctx.selected_school_name

        text = (
            "تم العثور على الموظف: " + row["full_name"] + "\n"
            "الرقم الوطني: " + (row["national_id"] or "-") + "\n"
            "الرقم الوزاري: " + (row["ministerial_number"] or "-") + "\n"
            "المدرسة: " + school + "\n"
            "التخصص: " + spec + " | المصدر: " + source + "\n"
            "الهاتف: " + phone
        )

        self.info_label.config(text=text, foreground="darkgreen")
        messagebox.showinfo("نجاح", msg("EMPLOYEE_FOUND"))
''')

add("src/ui/acknowledgement_dialog.py", r'''import socket
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk, messagebox
from datetime import datetime

from src.core.app_context import AppContext
from src.core.constants import ACK_TEXT
from src.db.connection import get_connection


class AcknowledgementDialog(tk.Toplevel):
    def __init__(self, parent, ctx: AppContext):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.ctx = ctx
        self.title("إقرار بمسؤولية دقة البيانات")
        self.geometry("700x450")
        self.transient(parent)
        self.grab_set()
        self._build_ui()

    def _build_ui(self):
        main = ttk.Frame(self, padding=20)
        main.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True)

        ttk.Label(main, text="إقرار بمسؤولية دقة البيانات", font=("Arial", 13, "bold")).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 10))

        txt = tk.Text(main, height=12, wrap=tk.WORD, font=("Arial", 10), state=tk.DISABLED, bg="#fff8e1")
        txt.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True, pady=5)
        txt.config(state=tk.NORMAL)
        txt.insert(tk.END, ACK_TEXT)
        txt.config(state=tk.DISABLED)

        self.ack_var = tk.BooleanVar(value=False)
        chk = ttk.Checkbutton(
            main,
            text="لقد اطلعت على الملاحظة وأتعهد بدقة البيانات المدخلة.",
            variable=self.ack_var
        )
        chk.pack(anchor="w", pady=10)

        btns = ttk.Frame(main)
        btns.pack(fill=tk.X)

        ttk.Button(btns, text="متابعة", command=self._confirm).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5)
        ttk.Button(btns, text="خروج", command=self._cancel).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5)

    def _confirm(self):
        if not self.ack_var.get():
            messagebox.showwarning("تنبيه", "يجب تحديد خانة الإقرار للمتابعة.")
            return

        self.ctx.acknowledgement_given = True
        self._save_ack()
        self.destroy()

    def _cancel(self):
        self.ctx.acknowledgement_given = False
        self.destroy()

    def _save_ack(self):
        try:
            conn = get_connection()
            cur = conn.cursor()
            cur.execute("""
                INSERT INTO form_acknowledgments(user_id, school_id, note_version, acknowledged_at, hostname)
                VALUES (?, ?, '2026-v1', ?, ?)
            """, (
                self.ctx.current_user_id,
                self.ctx.selected_school_id,
                datetime.now().isoformat(),
                socket.gethostname()
            ))
            conn.commit()
            conn.close()
        except Exception:
            pass
''')

from tkinter import ttk
import webbrowser

from src.core.constants import (

)


class HelpWindow(tk.Toplevel):

        self.title("المساعدة والتواصل")

    def _build_ui(self):

        main.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True)

        ttk.Label(main, text="بيانات التواصل والدعم", font=("Arial", 12, "bold")).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 10))

        rows = [
            ("حقوق النشر:", COPYRIGHT),
            ("البريد:", CONTACT_EMAIL),
            ("الهاتف:", CONTACT_PHONE),
            ("واتساب:", WHATSAPP),
        ]

        for i, (label, value) in enumerate(rows):

        ttk.Separator(main, orient=tk.HORIZONTAL).pack(fill=tk.X, pady=10)

        ttk.Button(btns, text="الذكاء الاصطناعي", command=lambda: webbrowser.open(AI_URL)).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5)

        ttk.Button(btns, text="بريد", command=self._open_email).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5)

    def _open_whatsapp(self):
        webbrowser.open("https://wa.me/" + WHATSAPP)

    def _open_email(self):
        webbrowser.open("mailto:" + CONTACT_EMAIL + "?subject=استفسار نظام المراقبة 2026")
''')

# main entry

add("src/main.py", r'''import sys
import os

# Support PyInstaller frozen and normal execution
if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
    BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(sys.executable)
else:
    BASE_DIR = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".."))

if BASE_DIR not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, BASE_DIR)

from src.ui.login_window import LoginApp
from src.core.constants import APP_TITLE


def main():
    if sys.maxsize > 2**32:
        print("Warning: Running on 64-bit Python. For 32-bit build, use Python x86.")
    app = LoginApp()
    app.run()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
''')


# ----------------------------
# init db
# ----------------------------
add("scripts/init_db.py", r'''import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path


BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DB_PATH = BASE_DIR / "exam2026.db"
SCHEMA_PATH = BASE_DIR / "src" / "db" / "schema.sql"


def init():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(DB_PATH))
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    conn.executescript(SCHEMA_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

    cur = conn.cursor()

    cur.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO roles(role_code, role_name, description) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                ("ADMIN", "مدير النظام", "صلاحيات كاملة"))
    cur.execute("SELECT role_id FROM roles WHERE role_code = 'ADMIN'")
    role_id = cur.fetchone()["role_id"]

    cur.execute("""
        INSERT OR IGNORE INTO users(full_name, national_id, ministerial_number, role_id, is_active, approval_status)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    """, ("مستخدم تجريبي", "9881018940", "172571", role_id, 1, "APPROVED"))

    cur.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO directorates(directorate_name) VALUES (?)",
                ("مديرية التربية والتعليم",))
    cur.execute("SELECT directorate_id FROM directorates WHERE directorate_name = ?",
                ("مديرية التربية والتعليم",))
    directorate_id = cur.fetchone()["directorate_id"]

    cur.execute("""
        INSERT OR IGNORE INTO schools(school_name, national_code, directorate_id, phone, address)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    """, ("مدرسة تجريبية", "100001", directorate_id, "0771234567", "عمان"))
    cur.execute("SELECT school_id FROM schools WHERE school_name = ?", ("مدرسة تجريبية",))
    school_id = cur.fetchone()["school_id"]

    cur.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO forms(form_code, form_name, revision, effective_year) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                ("ELECTRONIC", "المراقبة الإلكترونية", "2026", 2026))
    cur.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO forms(form_code, form_name, revision, effective_year) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                ("QF71-4-6", "مدير ومديرات المدارس ورؤساء الأقسام والمشرفين", "Rev.b", 2026))
    cur.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO forms(form_code, form_name, revision, effective_year) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                ("QF71-4-9", "أسماء الموظفين والمعلمين والمعلمات الحاصلين على شهادات جامعية", "Rev.b", 2026))

    cur.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO exam_cycles(cycle_name, year, exam_type) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                ("الامتحان التكميلي", 2026, "تكميلي"))

    cur.execute("""
        INSERT OR IGNORE INTO subjects(canonical_name_pdf, name_en, category, normalized_key, source, is_pdf_canonical, status)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    """, ("أخرى", "Other", "افتراضي - قيمة Excel فارغة", "اخري", "EXCEL_EMPTY", 0, "ACTIVE"))
    cur.execute("SELECT subject_id FROM subjects WHERE canonical_name_pdf = ?", ("أخرى",))
    other_subject_id = cur.fetchone()["subject_id"]

    cur.execute("""
        INSERT OR IGNORE INTO employees(national_id, ministerial_number, full_name, school_id, specialization_id, mobile_phone, job_title)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    """, ("1234567890", "654321", "موظف تجريبي", school_id, other_subject_id, "0771234567", "معلم"))

    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    print("Database initialized:", DB_PATH)
    print("Login demo:")
    print("Ministerial: 172571")
    print("National: 9881018940")
    print("Employee demo national: 1234567890")
    print("Employee demo ministerial: 654321")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    init()
''')


# ----------------------------
# build exe 32-bit
# ----------------------------
add("scripts/build_exe_32bit.bat", r'''@echo off
setlocal

echo ============================================
echo Build ExamMonitoring2026 as 32-bit EXE
echo Must run with Python 32-bit / x86
echo ============================================

python -c "import struct,sys; bits=struct.calcsize('P')*8; print('Python architecture:', bits, 'bit'); sys.exit(0 if bits==32 else 1)"
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo.
    echo ERROR: You are not using 32-bit Python.
    echo Install Python 3.12 Windows installer 32-bit/x86 and retry.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

echo.
echo Installing dependencies...
pip install -r requirements.txt
pip install pyinstaller

echo.
echo Building EXE...
pyinstaller ^
  --onefile ^
  --windowed ^
  --name ExamMonitoring2026 ^
  --add-data "config;config" ^
  --add-data "src/db/schema.sql;src/db" ^
  src/main.py

if errorlevel 1 (
    echo.
    echo ERROR: Build failed.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

if exist exam2026.db (
    copy /Y exam2026.db dist\exam2026.db >nul
)

echo.
echo ============================================
echo Build complete.
echo Output: dist\ExamMonitoring2026.exe
echo ============================================
pause
''')


# ----------------------------
# write and zip
# ----------------------------
def write_project():
    if ROOT.exists():
        print(f"Removing existing folder: {ROOT}")
        import shutil
        shutil.rmtree(ROOT)

    for rel_path, content in FILES.items():
        file_path = ROOT / rel_path
        file_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        file_path.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")

    print(f"Project folder created: {ROOT}")


def make_zip():
    if ZIP_NAME.exists():
        ZIP_NAME.unlink()

    with zipfile.ZipFile(ZIP_NAME, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
        for path in ROOT.rglob("*"):
            if path.is_file():
                zf.write(path, path.relative_to(ROOT.parent))

    digest = hashlib.sha256(ZIP_NAME.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    SHA_NAME.write_text(f"{digest}  {ZIP_NAME.name}\n", encoding="utf-8")

    print(f"ZIP created: {ZIP_NAME}")
    print(f"SHA-256: {digest}")
    print(f"SHA file: {SHA_NAME}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    write_project()
    make_zip()
```

# 5) ملاحظة مهمة جدًا

هذا الباكج الحالي هو **نسخة أساس قابلة للتشغيل** وتحتوي:

- شاشة دخول Python 32-bit.
- رقم وزاري 6 أرقام أو رقم وطني 10 أرقام.
- 3 محاولات ثم إغلاق البرنامج.
- قاعدة SQLite.
- تحديد المدرسة إجباريًا قبل النماذج.
- جلب بيانات الموظف تلقائيًا بالرقم الوطني أو الوزاري.
- إقرار مسؤولية البيانات.
- شاشة مساعدة وتواصل.
- رسائل احترافية.
- تحقق من الهاتف الأردني: `076 / 077 / 078 / 079` وعدد 10 خانات.
- سكريبت بناء EXE 32-bit.

لكن **شاشات النماذج الكاملة الثلاثة**:

```text
المراقبة الإلكترونية
مراقبة المدراء QF71-4-6
الجامعات QF71-4-9
```

مع:

- طباعة PDF مطابقة.
- تصدير Excel محمي.
- المرفقات.
- الحفظ التلقائي الكامل للمسودات.
- إدارة المستخدمين والأذونات الكاملة.

هذه تحتاج مرحلة توليد إضافية لأن حجمها كبير جدًا.

إذا أردت، أستطيع في الرسالة التالية أن أسلّمك **الباكج الكامل للنماذج الثلاثة** على شكل مولّد إضافي يضيف هذه الشاشات داخل نفس المشروع.

### USER
ماريك بسكربت لانشاء الملفات 

### ASSISTANT
رأيي: **ممتاز جدًا**، وسكربت إنشاء الملفات هو أفضل طريقة للتسليم الآن، لأنه:

1. ينشئ المشروع كاملًا بضغطة تشغيل واحدة.
2. يولّد `ZIP` + `SHA-256` للتسليم.
3. يمنع فقدان الملفات أو اختلاط النسخ.
4. يصلح للامتحان التكميلي 2026 كـ “حزمة تسليم قابلة للتشغيل”.
5. يمكن لاحقًا تحويله إلى `EXE 32-bit` بواسطة سكريبت البناء المرفق داخل المشروع.

إليك **سكربت نهائي محسّن** ينشئ مشروع Python 32-bit سطح مكتب، ويشمل:

- شاشة دخول: رقم وزاري 6 أرقام أو رقم وطني 10 أرقام.
- 3 محاولات ثم إغلاق البرنامج.
- SQLite.
- Tkinter RTL.
- تحديد المدرسة إجباريًا قبل النماذج.
- جلب بيانات الموظف تلقائيًا من قاعدة البيانات أو Excel.
- التخصص: PDF أولًا بالاسم الصريح، وإن لم يوجد فمن Excel كما هو، والفارغ = أخرى.
- قوائم بحث احترافية.
- حفظ تلقائي للمسودات.
- طباعة HTML قابلة للتحويل إلى PDF من المتصفح.
- تصدير Excel، والإلكتروني محمي.
- إقرار مسؤولية البيانات.
- رسائل احترافية.
- شاشة مساعدة وتواصل.
- سكريبت بناء EXE 32-bit.
- اختبارات أساسية.

# =========================================================
# arabic utils
# =========================================================
add("src/core/arabic_utils.py", r'''import re
import unicodedata


ARABIC_DIACRITICS = re.compile(r"[\u064B-\u0652\u0670\u06D6-\u06ED\u0640]")
NON_WORD = re.compile(r"[^\u0600-\u06FFa-z0-9\s]", re.IGNORECASE)
SPACES = re.compile(r"\s+")

NORMALIZE_MAP = str.maketrans({
    "أ": "ا",
    "إ": "ا",
    "آ": "ا",
    "ٱ": "ا",
    "ة": "ه",
    "ى": "ي",
    "ؤ": "و",
    "ئ": "ي",
})

STOPWORDS_AR = {"ماده", "تخصص", "اختياري", "اختصاص", "متقدم", "عامه", "اساسيه"}
STOPWORDS_EN = {"language", "optional", "for", "subject", "advanced"}


def normalize_arabic(text):
    if text is None:
        return ""

    text = str(text).strip()
    if not text:
        return ""

    text = unicodedata.normalize("NFKC", text).lower()
    text = ARABIC_DIACRITICS.sub("", text)
    text = text.translate(NORMALIZE_MAP)
    text = NON_WORD.sub(" ", text)
    text = SPACES.sub(" ", text).strip()

    words = []
    for w in text.split():
        if w in STOPWORDS_EN or w in STOPWORDS_AR:
            continue
        if len(w) > 3 and w.startswith("ال") and re.match(r"^[\u0600-\u06FF]", w):
            w = w[2:]
        words.append(w)

    return " ".join(words).strip()
''')


# =========================================================
# phone utils
# =========================================================
add("src/core/phone_utils.py", r'''import re
from src.core.constants import PHONE_REGEX, PHONE_PREFIXES


def normalize_jordan_mobile(value):
    if value is None:
        return ""

    digits = re.sub(r"\D", "", str(value))

    if digits.startswith("962") and len(digits) >= 12:
        digits = "0" + digits[3:]

    if len(digits) == 9 and re.match(r"^7[6789]", digits):
        digits = "0" + digits

    return digits


def is_valid_jordan_mobile(value):
    normalized = normalize_jordan_mobile(value)
    return bool(re.match(PHONE_REGEX, normalized))


def validate_phone_field(value):
    normalized = normalize_jordan_mobile(value)

    if not normalized:
        return False, "رقم الهاتف مطلوب."

    if len(normalized) != 10:
        return False, "رقم الهاتف يجب أن يتكون من 10 خانات."

    if not normalized.startswith(PHONE_PREFIXES):
        return False, "رقم الهاتف يجب أن يبدأ بـ 076 أو 077 أو 078 أو 079."

    if not re.match(PHONE_REGEX, normalized):
        return False, "رقم الهاتف غير صالح."

    return True, normalized
''')


# =========================================================
# validators
# =========================================================
add("src/core/validators.py", r'''import re
from src.core.constants import MINISTERIAL_LENGTH, NATIONAL_LENGTH
from src.core.phone_utils import validate_phone_field


def detect_login_type(value):
    value = str(value or "").strip()
    if not value.isdigit():
        return None
    if len(value) == MINISTERIAL_LENGTH:
        return "MINISTERIAL"
    if len(value) == NATIONAL_LENGTH:
        return "NATIONAL"
    return None


def validate_national_id(value):
    return bool(re.match(r"^\d{10}$", str(value or "").strip()))


def validate_ministerial_number(value):
    return bool(re.match(r"^\d{6}$", str(value or "").strip()))


def validate_required(text, label):
    if not text or not str(text).strip():
        return False, label + " مطلوب."
    return True, str(text).strip()


def validate_before_submit(data):
    errors = []

    if not data.get("school_id"):
        errors.append("يجب تحديد المدرسة.")

    if not data.get("employee_id"):
        errors.append("يجب اختيار موظف صالح.")

    ok, phone_msg = validate_phone_field(data.get("mobile_phone", ""))
    if not ok:
        errors.append(phone_msg)

    if not data.get("acknowledgement"):
        errors.append("يجب الإقرار بمسؤولية دقة البيانات.")

    return errors
''')


# =========================================================
# app context
# =========================================================
add("src/core/app_context.py", r'''from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any


@dataclass
class AppContext:
    current_user_id: Optional[int] = None
    current_user_name: str = ""
    current_role: str = ""

    selected_school_id: Optional[int] = None
    selected_school_name: str = ""

    selected_employee_id: Optional[int] = None
    selected_employee_name: str = ""
    selected_employee_data: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    active_form_id: Optional[int] = None
    active_form_code: str = ""

    failed_login_attempts: int = 0
    max_login_attempts: int = 3

    acknowledgement_given: bool = False

    drafts: dict = field(default_factory=dict)

    def require_school(self):
        return self.selected_school_id is not None

    def require_employee(self):
        return self.selected_employee_id is not None

    def reset_employee(self):
        self.selected_employee_id = None
        self.selected_employee_name = ""
        self.selected_employee_data = {}
''')


# =========================================================
# subject policy
# =========================================================
add("src/core/subject_policy.py", r'''import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict


DEFAULT_POLICY_PATH = Path("config/subject_policy.json")


@dataclass
class SubjectPolicy:
    pdf_matching: str = "ALIASES_PLUS_FUZZY"
    fuzzy_threshold: float = 94.0

    excel_fallback: str = "AS_IS"
    excel_fallback_status: str = "ACTIVE"
    excel_fallback_log: bool = True

    pdf_first_sort: bool = True

    empty_excel_mode: str = "OTHER"
    empty_excel_display_name: str = "أخرى"
    empty_excel_source_label: str = "EXCEL_EMPTY"
    empty_excel_status: str = "ACTIVE"
    empty_excel_log: bool = True

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, path=DEFAULT_POLICY_PATH):
        if not Path(path).exists():
            return cls()

        data = json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

        return cls(
            pdf_matching=data.get("pdf_matching", cls.pdf_matching),
            fuzzy_threshold=float(data.get("fuzzy_threshold", cls.fuzzy_threshold)),

            excel_fallback=data.get("excel_fallback", cls.excel_fallback),
            excel_fallback_status=data.get("excel_fallback_status", cls.excel_fallback_status),
            excel_fallback_log=bool(data.get("excel_fallback_log", cls.excel_fallback_log)),

            pdf_first_sort=bool(data.get("pdf_first_sort", cls.pdf_first_sort)),

            empty_excel_mode=data.get("empty_excel_mode", cls.empty_excel_mode),
            empty_excel_display_name=data.get("empty_excel_display_name", cls.empty_excel_display_name),
            empty_excel_source_label=data.get("empty_excel_source_label", cls.empty_excel_source_label),
            empty_excel_status=data.get("empty_excel_status", cls.empty_excel_status),
            empty_excel_log=bool(data.get("empty_excel_log", cls.empty_excel_log)),
        )
''')


# =========================================================
# subject resolver
# =========================================================
add("src/core/subject_resolver.py", r'''import math
import sqlite3
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, List, Tuple

from src.core.arabic_utils import normalize_arabic
from src.core.subject_policy import SubjectPolicy


try:
    from rapidfuzz import fuzz
    HAVE_FUZZY = True
except ImportError:
    HAVE_FUZZY = False


@dataclass
class SubjectResolution:
    subject_id: Optional[int]
    display_name: str
    source: str
    matched_pdf: bool
    raw_value: str
    confidence: float
    status: str
    message: str


class SubjectResolver:
    def __init__(self, conn, policy=None):
        self.conn = conn
        self.conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        self.policy = policy or SubjectPolicy.load()

        self.raw_pdf_map = {}
        self.normalized_pdf_map = {}
        self.pdf_search_strings = []

        self._load_pdf_maps()

    def _load_pdf_maps(self):
        cur = self.conn.cursor()

        cur.execute("""
            SELECT subject_id, canonical_name_pdf, normalized_key
            FROM subjects
            WHERE is_active = 1
              AND (source = 'PDF' OR is_pdf_canonical = 1)
              AND COALESCE(status, 'ACTIVE') = 'ACTIVE'
        """)

        for row in cur.fetchall():
            sid = row["subject_id"]
            canonical = row["canonical_name_pdf"] or ""
            normalized = row["normalized_key"] or normalize_arabic(canonical)

            if canonical:
                self.raw_pdf_map.setdefault(canonical, sid)
                self.pdf_search_strings.append((sid, canonical, normalized))

            if normalized:
                self.normalized_pdf_map.setdefault(normalized, sid)

        cur.execute("""
            SELECT sa.subject_id, sa.alias_text, sa.normalized_alias
            FROM subject_aliases sa
            JOIN subjects s ON s.subject_id = sa.subject_id
            WHERE s.is_active = 1
              AND (s.source = 'PDF' OR s.is_pdf_canonical = 1)
              AND COALESCE(s.status, 'ACTIVE') = 'ACTIVE'
        """)

        for row in cur.fetchall():
            sid = row["subject_id"]
            alias = row["alias_text"] or ""
            normalized_alias = row["normalized_alias"] or normalize_arabic(alias)

            if alias:
                self.raw_pdf_map.setdefault(alias, sid)

            if normalized_alias:
                self.normalized_pdf_map.setdefault(normalized_alias, sid)
                self.pdf_search_strings.append((sid, alias, normalized_alias))

    def resolve(self, raw_value):
        raw = self._clean_raw_value(raw_value)

        if not raw:
            if self.policy.empty_excel_mode == "OTHER":
                subject_id = self._get_or_create_other_subject()

                if self.policy.empty_excel_log:
                    self._log_audit(
                        "subjects",
                        subject_id,
                        "EXCEL_EMPTY_DEFAULT_OTHER",
                        "display_name=" + self.policy.empty_excel_display_name +
                        "; source=" + self.policy.empty_excel_source_label +
                        "; status=" + self.policy.empty_excel_status
                    )

                return SubjectResolution(
                    subject_id=subject_id,
                    display_name=self.policy.empty_excel_display_name,
                    source=self.policy.empty_excel_source_label,
                    matched_pdf=False,
                    raw_value="",
                    confidence=0.0,
                    status=self.policy.empty_excel_status,
                    message="قيمة التخصص فارغة في Excel، تم اعتمادها كـ (أخرى)."
                )

            return SubjectResolution(
                subject_id=None,
                display_name="",
                source="EMPTY",
                matched_pdf=False,
                raw_value="",
                confidence=0.0,
                status="NONE",
                message="قيمة التخصص فارغة في Excel."
            )

        subject_id = self.raw_pdf_map.get(raw)
        if subject_id:
            display = self._get_display_name(subject_id)
            return SubjectResolution(
                subject_id=subject_id,
                display_name=display,
                source="PDF",
                matched_pdf=True,
                raw_value=raw,
                confidence=100.0,
                status="ACTIVE",
                message="تمت مطابقة التخصص مع PDF حرفيًا."
            )

        normalized_raw = normalize_arabic(raw)

        if normalized_raw:
            subject_id = self.normalized_pdf_map.get(normalized_raw)
            if subject_id:
                display = self._get_display_name(subject_id)
                return SubjectResolution(
                    subject_id=subject_id,
                    display_name=display,
                    source="PDF",
                    matched_pdf=True,
                    raw_value=raw,
                    confidence=100.0,
                    status="ACTIVE",
                    message="تمت مطابقة التخصص مع PDF بعد التطبيع العربي."
                )

        if self.policy.pdf_matching == "ALIASES_PLUS_FUZZY" and HAVE_FUZZY and normalized_raw:
            fuzzy_match = self._fuzzy_pdf_match(normalized_raw)
            if fuzzy_match:
                subject_id, score = fuzzy_match
                display = self._get_display_name(subject_id)
                return SubjectResolution(
                    subject_id=subject_id,
                    display_name=display,
                    source="PDF",
                    matched_pdf=True,
                    raw_value=raw,
                    confidence=score,
                    status="ACTIVE",
                    message="تمت مطابقة التخصص مع PDF مطابقة ذكية عالية الدقة."
                )

        if self.policy.excel_fallback != "AS_IS":
            return SubjectResolution(
                subject_id=None,
                display_name=raw,
                source="NOT_FOUND",
                matched_pdf=False,
                raw_value=raw,
                confidence=0.0,
                status="NOT_FOUND",
                message="التخصص غير موجود في PDF، ولا يسمح النظام باستخدام Excel كاحتياطي."
            )

        subject_id = self._get_or_create_excel_subject(raw)
        status = self.policy.excel_fallback_status or "ACTIVE"

        message = (
            "التخصص غير موجود في PDF، تم اعتماده من Excel كما هو."
            if status == "ACTIVE"
            else "التخصص غير موجود في PDF، تم استيراده من Excel كما هو وهو قيد المراجعة."
        )

        return SubjectResolution(
            subject_id=subject_id,
            display_name=raw,
            source="EXCEL",
            matched_pdf=False,
            raw_value=raw,
            confidence=0.0,
            status=status,
            message=message
        )

    def _clean_raw_value(self, raw_value):
        if raw_value is None:
            return ""

        try:
            if isinstance(raw_value, float) and math.isnan(raw_value):
                return ""
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            if raw_value != raw_value:
                return ""
        except Exception:
            pass

        text = str(raw_value).strip()

        if text.lower() in {"nan", "none", "null", "nat", ""}:
            return ""

        return text

    def _fuzzy_pdf_match(self, normalized_raw):
        if not HAVE_FUZZY:
            return None

        best_id = None
        best_score = 0.0
        threshold = float(self.policy.fuzzy_threshold or 94.0)

        for sid, text, normalized_text in self.pdf_search_strings:
            if not normalized_text:
                continue

            w_ratio = fuzz.WRatio(normalized_raw, normalized_text)
            token_sort = fuzz.token_sort_ratio(normalized_raw, normalized_text)
            score = max(w_ratio, token_sort)

            if score >= threshold and score > best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_id = sid

        if best_id is not None:
            return best_id, round(best_score, 2)

        return None

    def _get_display_name(self, subject_id):
        cur = self.conn.cursor()
        cur.execute("SELECT canonical_name_pdf FROM subjects WHERE subject_id = ?", (subject_id,))
        row = cur.fetchone()
        return row["canonical_name_pdf"] if row else ""

    def _get_or_create_excel_subject(self, raw):
        cur = self.conn.cursor()

        cur.execute("""
            SELECT subject_id
            FROM subjects
            WHERE canonical_name_pdf = ?
              AND source = 'EXCEL'
        """, (raw,))

        row = cur.fetchone()
        if row:
            subject_id = row["subject_id"]

            cur.execute("""
                UPDATE subjects
                SET status = ?,
                    needs_review = ?,
                    raw_excel_value = ?
                WHERE subject_id = ?
            """, (
                self.policy.excel_fallback_status,
                1 if self.policy.excel_fallback_status == "PENDING" else 0,
                raw,
                subject_id
            ))

            self.conn.commit()
            return subject_id

        cur.execute("""
            SELECT subject_id
            FROM subjects
            WHERE canonical_name_pdf = ?
        """, (raw,))

        row = cur.fetchone()
        if row:
            return row["subject_id"]

        normalized_key = normalize_arabic(raw)

        cur.execute("""
            INSERT INTO subjects (
                canonical_name_pdf,
                name_en,
                category,
                grade_levels,
                normalized_key,
                is_active,
                source,
                is_pdf_canonical,
                raw_excel_value,
                needs_review,
                status,
                created_at
            )
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (
            raw,
            None,
            "من Excel - غير موجود في PDF",
            None,
            normalized_key,
            1,
            "EXCEL",
            0,
            raw,
            1 if self.policy.excel_fallback_status == "PENDING" else 0,
            self.policy.excel_fallback_status,
            datetime.now().isoformat()
        ))

        subject_id = cur.lastrowid
        self.conn.commit()

        if self.policy.excel_fallback_log:
            self._log_audit(
                "subjects",
                subject_id,
                "EXCEL_FALLBACK_CREATE",
                "canonical_name_pdf=" + raw + "; source=EXCEL; status=" + self.policy.excel_fallback_status
            )

        return subject_id

    def _get_or_create_other_subject(self):
        cur = self.conn.cursor()
        other_name = self.policy.empty_excel_display_name or "أخرى"

        cur.execute("""
            SELECT subject_id
            FROM subjects
            WHERE canonical_name_pdf = ?
        """, (other_name,))

        row = cur.fetchone()
        if row:
            subject_id = row["subject_id"]

            cur.execute("""
                UPDATE subjects
                SET status = ?,
                    needs_review = ?
                WHERE subject_id = ?
            """, (
                self.policy.empty_excel_status,
                1 if self.policy.empty_excel_status == "PENDING" else 0,
                subject_id
            ))

            self.conn.commit()
            return subject_id

        normalized_key = normalize_arabic(other_name)

        cur.execute("""
            INSERT INTO subjects (
                canonical_name_pdf,
                name_en,
                category,
                grade_levels,
                normalized_key,
                is_active,
                source,
                is_pdf_canonical,
                raw_excel_value,
                needs_review,
                status,
                created_at
            )
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (
            other_name,
            "Other",
            "افتراضي - قيمة Excel فارغة",
            None,
            normalized_key,
            1,
            self.policy.empty_excel_source_label,
            0,
            None,
            1 if self.policy.empty_excel_status == "PENDING" else 0,
            self.policy.empty_excel_status,
            datetime.now().isoformat()
        ))

        subject_id = cur.lastrowid
        self.conn.commit()
        return subject_id

    def _log_audit(self, table_name, record_id, action, new_values):
        try:
            cur = self.conn.cursor()
            cur.execute("""
                INSERT INTO audit_logs (
                    table_name,
                    record_id,
                    action,
                    new_values,
                    created_at
                )
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (
                table_name,
                record_id,
                action,
                new_values,
                datetime.now().isoformat()
            ))
            self.conn.commit()
        except sqlite3.OperationalError:
            pass
''')


# =========================================================
# db connection
# =========================================================
add("src/db/connection.py", r'''import sqlite3
import sys
from pathlib import Path


if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
    BASE_DIR = Path(sys.executable).parent
else:
    BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

DB_PATH = BASE_DIR / "exam2026.db"


def get_connection(db_path=DB_PATH):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(db_path))
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON")
    conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL")
    return conn
''')


# =========================================================
# schema
# =========================================================
add("src/db/schema.sql", r'''PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS settings (
    setting_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
    setting_value TEXT,
    description TEXT,
    updated_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS roles (
    role_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    role_code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    role_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    is_manual INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    created_by INTEGER,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS permissions (
    permission_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    permission_code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    permission_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    module TEXT,
    is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    is_manual INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
    created_by INTEGER,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    approved_by INTEGER,
    approved_at TEXT
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS role_permissions (
    role_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    permission_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    granted_by INTEGER,
    granted_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    PRIMARY KEY (role_id, permission_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (role_id) REFERENCES roles(role_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (permission_id) REFERENCES permissions(permission_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
    user_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    full_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    national_id TEXT UNIQUE,
    ministerial_number TEXT UNIQUE,
    role_id INTEGER,
    password_hash TEXT,
    is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    failed_login_attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    locked_until TEXT,
    last_login_at TEXT,
    created_manually INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    manual_reason TEXT,
    requested_by INTEGER,
    approved_by INTEGER,
    approval_status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'APPROVED',
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TEXT,
    FOREIGN KEY (role_id) REFERENCES roles(role_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS login_attempts (
    attempt_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    login_value TEXT,
    detected_type TEXT,
    success INTEGER NOT NULL,
    message TEXT,
    hostname TEXT,
    machine_user TEXT,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS audit_logs (
    audit_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    table_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    record_id INTEGER,
    action TEXT NOT NULL,
    old_values TEXT,
    new_values TEXT,
    user_id INTEGER,
    hostname TEXT,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS directorates (
    directorate_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    directorate_name TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schools (
    school_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    school_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    normalized_name TEXT,
    national_code TEXT,
    directorate_id INTEGER,
    branch_name TEXT,
    section_name TEXT,
    phone TEXT,
    address TEXT,
    is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    FOREIGN KEY (directorate_id) REFERENCES directorates(directorate_id)
);

CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_schools_national_code
ON schools(national_code) WHERE national_code IS NOT NULL;

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_schools_name ON schools(school_name);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_schools_normalized_name ON schools(normalized_name);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS subjects (
    subject_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    canonical_name_pdf TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    name_en TEXT,
    category TEXT,
    grade_levels TEXT,
    normalized_key TEXT NOT NULL,
    is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    source TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PDF',
    is_pdf_canonical INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    raw_excel_value TEXT,
    needs_review INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_subjects_source ON subjects(source);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_subjects_status ON subjects(status);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_subjects_is_pdf_canonical ON subjects(is_pdf_canonical);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS subject_aliases (
    alias_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    subject_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    alias_text TEXT NOT NULL,
    normalized_alias TEXT NOT NULL,
    source TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'manual',
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    FOREIGN KEY (subject_id) REFERENCES subjects(subject_id),
    UNIQUE(normalized_alias)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS employees (
    employee_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    national_id TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    ministerial_number TEXT UNIQUE,
    full_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    gender TEXT,
    job_title TEXT,
    directorate_id INTEGER,
    school_id INTEGER,
    specialization_id INTEGER,
    years_experience INTEGER,
    grade TEXT,
    residence TEXT,
    mobile_phone TEXT NOT NULL,
    university_name TEXT,
    is_new_employee INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    notes TEXT,
    excel_source_file TEXT,
    excel_row_number INTEGER,
    last_auto_filled_at TEXT,
    created_by INTEGER,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TEXT,
    FOREIGN KEY (specialization_id) REFERENCES subjects(subject_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (school_id) REFERENCES schools(school_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (directorate_id) REFERENCES directorates(directorate_id)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_employees_national ON employees(national_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_employees_ministerial ON employees(ministerial_number);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS excel_employee_import (
    import_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    source_file TEXT NOT NULL,
    sheet_name TEXT,
    row_number INTEGER,
    national_id TEXT,
    ministerial_number TEXT,
    full_name TEXT,
    school_name TEXT,
    school_national_code TEXT,
    directorate_name TEXT,
    specialization_raw TEXT,
    resolved_specialization_id INTEGER,
    specialization_source TEXT,
    mobile_phone_raw TEXT,
    normalized_mobile_phone TEXT,
    years_experience INTEGER,
    grade TEXT,
    residence TEXT,
    university_name TEXT,
    is_new_employee INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    notes TEXT,
    raw_json TEXT NOT NULL,
    imported_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    FOREIGN KEY (resolved_specialization_id) REFERENCES subjects(subject_id)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_excel_import_national ON excel_employee_import(national_id);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_excel_import_ministerial ON excel_employee_import(ministerial_number);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS forms (
    form_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    form_code TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
    form_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    revision TEXT,
    effective_year INTEGER,
    is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS exam_cycles (
    cycle_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    cycle_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    year INTEGER NOT NULL,
    exam_type TEXT,
    start_date TEXT,
    end_date TEXT,
    is_active INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS submissions (
    submission_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    form_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    employee_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    cycle_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    school_id INTEGER,
    directorate_id INTEGER,
    status INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    is_locked INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    locked_at TEXT,
    locked_by INTEGER,
    entered_by INTEGER NOT NULL,
    entered_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    approved_by INTEGER,
    approved_at TEXT,
    approval_note TEXT,
    rejection_reason TEXT,
    pdf_path TEXT,
    excel_path TEXT,
    form_data TEXT,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TEXT,
    FOREIGN KEY (form_id) REFERENCES forms(form_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (employee_id) REFERENCES employees(employee_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (cycle_id) REFERENCES exam_cycles(cycle_id),
    UNIQUE (employee_id, form_id, cycle_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS drafts (
    draft_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    form_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    employee_id INTEGER,
    school_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    cycle_id INTEGER,
    user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT',
    json_data TEXT NOT NULL,
    created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    FOREIGN KEY (form_id) REFERENCES forms(form_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (employee_id) REFERENCES employees(employee_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (school_id) REFERENCES schools(school_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(user_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS form_acknowledgments (
    acknowledgment_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    school_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
    employee_id INTEGER,
    form_id INTEGER,
    submission_id INTEGER,
    note_version TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '2026-v1',
    acknowledged_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    hostname TEXT,
    FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(user_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (school_id) REFERENCES schools(school_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS attachments (
    attachment_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    submission_id INTEGER,
    employee_id INTEGER,
    file_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    original_file_name TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_path TEXT NOT NULL,
    mime_type TEXT,
    file_size INTEGER,
    category TEXT NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    uploaded_by INTEGER NOT NULL,
    uploaded_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    FOREIGN KEY (submission_id) REFERENCES submissions(submission_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (employee_id) REFERENCES employees(employee_id)
);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS exports (
    export_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    submission_id INTEGER,
    report_name TEXT,
    export_type TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_path TEXT NOT NULL,
    file_hash TEXT,
    is_protected INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    protection_level TEXT,
    generated_by INTEGER NOT NULL,
    generated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    FOREIGN KEY (submission_id) REFERENCES submissions(submission_id)
);
''')


# =========================================================
