add("src/services/audit_service.py", r'''import socket
from datetime import datetime


def log_audit(conn, table_name, record_id, action, old_values=None, new_values=None, user_id=None):
    try:
        cur = conn.cursor()
        cur.execute("""
            INSERT INTO audit_logs(table_name, record_id, action, old_values, new_values, user_id, hostname, created_at)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (
            table_name,
            record_id,
            action,
            old_values,
            new_values,
            user_id,
            socket.gethostname(),
            datetime.now().isoformat()
        ))
        conn.commit()
    except Exception:
        pass
''')


# =========================================================
# draft service
# =========================================================
add("src/services/draft_service.py", r'''import json
from datetime import datetime


def get_form_id(conn, form_code):
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute("SELECT form_id FROM forms WHERE form_code = ?", (form_code,))
    row = cur.fetchone()
    return row["form_id"] if row else None


def get_cycle_id(conn, year=2026):
    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute("SELECT cycle_id FROM exam_cycles WHERE year = ?", (year,))
    row = cur.fetchone()
    return row["cycle_id"] if row else None


def save_draft(conn, form_code, ctx, data):
    form_id = get_form_id(conn, form_code)
    cycle_id = get_cycle_id(conn)

    if not form_id or not ctx.selected_school_id or not ctx.current_user_id:
        return None

    cur = conn.cursor()
    payload = json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False)

    cur.execute("""
        SELECT draft_id
        FROM drafts
        WHERE form_id = ?
          AND school_id = ?
          AND user_id = ?
          AND (employee_id IS ? OR employee_id = ?)
          AND status = 'DRAFT'
    """, (form_id, ctx.selected_school_id, ctx.current_user_id, ctx.selected_employee_id, ctx.selected_employee_id))

    row = cur.fetchone()

    if row:
        draft_id = row["draft_id"]
        cur.execute("""
            UPDATE drafts
            SET json_data = ?, updated_at = ?
            WHERE draft_id = ?
        """, (payload, datetime.now().isoformat(), draft_id))
    else:
        cur.execute("""
            INSERT INTO drafts(form_id, employee_id, school_id, cycle_id, user_id, status, json_data)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'DRAFT', ?)
        """, (form_id, ctx.selected_employee_id, ctx.selected_school_id, cycle_id, ctx.current_user_id, payload))
        draft_id = cur.lastrowid

    conn.commit()
    return draft_id


def load_draft(conn, form_code, ctx):
    form_id = get_form_id(conn, form_code)
    if not form_id:
        return None

    cur = conn.cursor()
    cur.execute("""
        SELECT json_data
        FROM drafts
        WHERE form_id = ?
          AND school_id = ?
          AND user_id = ?
          AND (employee_id IS ? OR employee_id = ?)
          AND status = 'DRAFT'
        ORDER BY updated_at DESC
        LIMIT 1
    """, (form_id, ctx.selected_school_id, ctx.current_user_id, ctx.selected_employee_id, ctx.selected_employee_id))

    row = cur.fetchone()
    if not row:
        return None

    try:
        return json.loads(row["json_data"])
    except Exception:
        return None
''')


# =========================================================
# employee auto fill service
# =========================================================
add("src/services/employee_auto_fill_service.py", r'''from datetime import datetime

from src.core.validators import detect_login_type
from src.core.phone_utils import normalize_jordan_mobile
from src.core.subject_resolver import SubjectResolver


def find_or_create_employee(conn, value, school_id, user_id=None):
    login_type = detect_login_type(value)
    if not login_type:
        return None, "رقم غير صالح"

    col = "ministerial_number" if login_type == "MINISTERIAL" else "national_id"
    cur = conn.cursor()

    cur.execute("""
        SELECT e.*, s.canonical_name_pdf AS specialization_name, s.source AS specialization_source
        FROM employees e
        LEFT JOIN subjects s ON s.subject_id = e.specialization_id
        WHERE e.{0} = ?
          AND e.school_id = ?
    """.format(col), (value, school_id))

    row = cur.fetchone()
    if row:
        return dict(row), None

    cur.execute("""
        SELECT *
        FROM excel_employee_import
        WHERE {0} = ?
        ORDER BY import_id DESC
        LIMIT 1
    """.format(col), (value,))

    excel_row = cur.fetchone()
    if not excel_row:
        return None, "لم يتم العثور على موظف"

    resolver = SubjectResolver(conn)
    resolution = resolver.resolve(excel_row["specialization_raw"])

    full_name = excel_row["full_name"] or "غير معروف"
    phone = normalize_jordan_mobile(excel_row["normalized_mobile_phone"] or excel_row["mobile_phone_raw"] or "")

    try:
        cur.execute("""
            INSERT INTO employees(
                national_id,
                ministerial_number,
                full_name,
                school_id,
                specialization_id,
                mobile_phone,
                years_experience,
                grade,
                residence,
                university_name,
                is_new_employee,
                notes,
                excel_source_file,
                excel_row_number,
                last_auto_filled_at,
                created_by
            )
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (
            excel_row["national_id"],
            excel_row["ministerial_number"],
            full_name,
            school_id,
            resolution.subject_id,
            phone,
            excel_row["years_experience"],
            excel_row["grade"],
            excel_row["residence"],
            excel_row["university_name"],
            excel_row["is_new_employee"] or 0,
            excel_row["notes"],
            excel_row["source_file"],
            excel_row["row_number"],
            datetime.now().isoformat(),
            user_id
        ))
        conn.commit()
        employee_id = cur.lastrowid
    except Exception:
        conn.rollback()
        cur.execute("SELECT employee_id FROM employees WHERE {0} = ?".format(col), (value,))
        existing = cur.fetchone()
        if not existing:
            return None, "تعذر إنشاء سجل الموظف من Excel"
        employee_id = existing["employee_id"]

    cur.execute("""
        SELECT e.*, s.canonical_name_pdf AS specialization_name, s.source AS specialization_source
        FROM employees e
        LEFT JOIN subjects s ON s.subject_id = e.specialization_id
        WHERE e.employee_id = ?
    """, (employee_id,))

    final = cur.fetchone()
    return dict(final), None
''')


# =========================================================
# export service
# =========================================================
add("src/services/export_service.py", r'''import os
import html
import webbrowser
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

from openpyxl import Workbook
from openpyxl.styles import Font, Alignment
from openpyxl.worksheet.protection import SheetProtection

from src.core.constants import APP_TITLE, COPYRIGHT, ORG_NAME, DEPT_NAME, FORM_ELECTRONIC
from src.db.connection import BASE_DIR


EXPORT_DIR = BASE_DIR / "exports"


def _ensure_dirs():
    (EXPORT_DIR / "pdf").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (EXPORT_DIR / "excel").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)


def _value_to_text(value):
    if value is None:
        return ""
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        return "نعم" if value else "لا"
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return value.get("display") or value.get("name") or str(value)
    return str(value)


def print_form_html(form_code, form_title, data, ctx, locked=False):
    _ensure_dirs()

    ts = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    file_path = EXPORT_DIR / "pdf" / ("{0}_{1}.html".format(form_code, ts))

    status_text = "مقدم ومقفول" if locked else "مسودة غير معتمدة"

    rows = []
    for key, value in data.items():
        rows.append(
            "<tr><td style='padding:6px;border:1px solid #ddd;font-weight:bold'>{0}</td>"
            "<td style='padding:6px;border:1px solid #ddd'>{1}</td></tr>".format(
                html.escape(str(key)),
                html.escape(_value_to_text(value))
            )
        )

    logo_tag = ""
    logo_path = Path("assets/branding/logo-modern.png")
    if logo_path.exists():
        logo_tag = "<img src='{0}' style='max-height:90px;max-width:160px'/>".format(logo_path.as_posix())

    content = """<!doctype html>
<html dir="rtl" lang="ar">
<head>
<meta charset="utf-8">
<title>{title}</title>
<style>
body {{ font-family: Arial, sans-serif; margin: 24px; color: #111; }}
.header {{ display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center; border-bottom:2px solid #1f2937; padding-bottom:12px; margin-bottom:16px; }}
.title {{ font-size:20px; font-weight:bold; }}
.meta {{ color:#555; }}
table {{ width:100%; border-collapse:collapse; margin-top:12px; }}
.footer {{ margin-top:24px; border-top:1px solid #ddd; padding-top:10px; color:#555; font-size:13px; }}
.badge {{ background:#fee2e2; color:#991b1b; padding:4px 8px; border-radius:6px; font-weight:bold; }}
.badge.ok {{ background:#dcfce7; color:#166534; }}
@media print {{ body {{ margin: 10mm; }} }}
</style>
</head>
<body>
<div class="header">
  <div>
    <div class="title">{org}<br>{dept}</div>
    <div class="meta">{app_title}</div>
  </div>
  <div>{logo}</div>
</div>

<h2>{form_title} <span class="badge {'ok' if locked else ''}">{status}</span></h2>
<div class="meta">المدرسة: {school} | الموظف: {employee} | المستخدم: {user} | التاريخ: {now}</div>

<table>
<tbody>
{rows}
</tbody>
</table>

<div class="footer">
{copyright}<br>
للدعم الفني: nsnmsos1988@gmail.com | +962 778 456 655
</div>

<script>window.onload=function(){{setTimeout(function(){{window.print();}},300);}};</script>
</body>
</html>
""".format(
        title=html.escape(form_title),
        org=html.escape(ORG_NAME),
        dept=html.escape(DEPT_NAME),
        app_title=html.escape(APP_TITLE),
        logo=logo_tag,
        form_title=html.escape(form_title),
        status=html.escape(status_text),
        school=html.escape(ctx.selected_school_name or ""),
        employee=html.escape(ctx.selected_employee_name or ""),
        user=html.escape(ctx.current_user_name or ""),
        now=datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M"),
        rows="\n".join(rows),
        copyright=html.escape(COPYRIGHT)
    )

    file_path.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
    webbrowser.open(file_path.as_uri())
    return str(file_path)


def export_excel(form_code, form_title, data, ctx, protected=False):
    _ensure_dirs()

    ts = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")
    file_path = EXPORT_DIR / "excel" / ("{0}_{1}.xlsx".format(form_code, ts))

    wb = Workbook()
    ws = wb.active
    ws.title = form_code[:31]

    ws.append(["الحقل", "القيمة"])
    ws["A1"].font = Font(bold=True)
    ws["B1"].font = Font(bold=True)

    for key, value in data.items():
        ws.append([str(key), _value_to_text(value)])

    ws.column_dimensions["A"].width = 35
    ws.column_dimensions["B"].width = 70

    for row in ws.iter_rows(min_row=2):
        for cell in row:
            cell.alignment = Alignment(wrap_text=True, vertical="top")

    if protected or form_code == FORM_ELECTRONIC:
        ws.protection = SheetProtection(
            sheet=True,
            formatCells=False,
            formatColumns=False,
            formatRows=False,
            insertColumns=False,
            insertRows=False,
            insertHyperlinks=False,
            deleteColumns=False,
            deleteRows=False,
            sort=False,
            autoFilter=False,
            pivotTables=False
        )

    wb.save(file_path)
    return str(file_path)
''')


# =========================================================
# searchable combo
# =========================================================
add("src/ui/searchable_combo.py", r'''import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk

from src.core.arabic_utils import normalize_arabic


class SearchableCombobox(ttk.Frame):
    def __init__(self, master, choices, command=None, width=40, placeholder="ابحث واختر..."):
        super().__init__(master)

        self.choices = list(choices)
        self.filtered_choices = list(self.choices)
        self.command = command
        self.selected_id = None

        self.var = tk.StringVar()
        self.placeholder = placeholder

        self.entry = ttk.Entry(self, textvariable=self.var, width=width)
        self.entry.pack(side=tk.LEFT, fill=tk.X, expand=True)

        self.dropdown = tk.Toplevel(self)
        self.dropdown.withdraw()
        self.dropdown.overrideredirect(True)

        self.listbox = tk.Listbox(
            self.dropdown,
            height=10,
            exportselection=False,
            activestyle="none",
            font=("Arial", 10)
        )
        self.listbox.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True)

        scrollbar = ttk.Scrollbar(self.dropdown, orient=tk.VERTICAL, command=self.listbox.yview)
        scrollbar.pack(side=tk.RIGHT, fill=tk.Y)
        self.listbox.config(yscrollcommand=scrollbar.set)

        self.no_result_label = ttk.Label(self.dropdown, text="لا توجد نتائج مطابقة.", foreground="red")

        self.entry.bind("<KeyRelease>", self._on_key_release)
        self.entry.bind("<FocusIn>", self._show_dropdown)
        self.entry.bind("<FocusOut>", self._on_focus_out)
        self.entry.bind("<Down>", self._open_dropdown)
        self.entry.bind("<Up>", self._open_dropdown)
        self.entry.bind("<Return>", self._select_current)
        self.entry.bind("<Escape>", self._hide_dropdown)

        self.listbox.bind("<ButtonRelease-1>", self._select_clicked)
        self.listbox.bind("<Return>", self._select_current)

        self.after_id = None
        self.hide_after_id = None

        self._refresh_listbox()

    def _refresh_listbox(self):
        self.listbox.delete(0, tk.END)
        for _, display in self.filtered_choices:
            self.listbox.insert(tk.END, display)

        if self.filtered_choices:
            self.listbox.selection_set(0)
            self.no_result_label.pack_forget()
        else:
            self.no_result_label.pack(side=tk.TOP, fill=tk.X, padx=5, pady=5)

    def _on_key_release(self, event=None):
        if self.after_id:
            self.after_cancel(self.after_id)
        self.after_id = self.after(220, self._filter_choices)

    def _filter_choices(self):
        raw_query = self.var.get().strip()
        query = normalize_arabic(raw_query)

        if not raw_query:
            self.filtered_choices = list(self.choices)
        else:
            self.filtered_choices = [
                choice for choice in self.choices
                if query in normalize_arabic(choice[1])
            ]

        self._refresh_listbox()
        self._show_dropdown()

    def _show_dropdown(self, event=None):
        if self.hide_after_id:
            self.after_cancel(self.hide_after_id)
            self.hide_after_id = None

        self.update_idletasks()
        x = self.winfo_rootx()
        y = self.winfo_rooty() + self.winfo_height()
        width = self.winfo_width()

        self.dropdown.geometry("{0}x220+{1}+{2}".format(width, x, y))
        self.dropdown.deiconify()
        self.dropdown.lift()

    def _open_dropdown(self, event=None):
        self._show_dropdown()
        if self.filtered_choices:
            self.listbox.selection_set(0)
        return "break"

    def _hide_dropdown(self, event=None):
        self.dropdown.withdraw()

    def _on_focus_out(self, event=None):
        self.hide_after_id = self.after(220, self._hide_dropdown)

    def _select_clicked(self, event=None):
        selection = self.listbox.curselection()
        if selection:
            self._set_choice(selection[0])
        self._hide_dropdown()

    def _select_current(self, event=None):
        selection = self.listbox.curselection()
        if selection:
            index = selection[0]
        elif self.filtered_choices:
            index = 0
        else:
            return

        self._set_choice(index)
        self._hide_dropdown()
        return "break"

    def _set_choice(self, index):
        if index < 0 or index >= len(self.filtered_choices):
            return

        selected_id, display = self.filtered_choices[index]
        self.selected_id = selected_id
        self.var.set(display)

        if self.command:
            self.command(selected_id, display)

    def set_selection(self, subject_id, display):
        self.selected_id = subject_id
        self.var.set(display or "")

    def get_selected_id(self):
        return self.selected_id

    def get_text(self):
        return self.var.get().strip()

    def clear(self):
        self.selected_id = None
        self.var.set("")

    def set_state(self, state):
        self.entry.config(state=state)
''')


# =========================================================
# scrollable frame
# =========================================================
add("src/ui/scrollable_frame.py", r'''import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk


class ScrollableFrame(ttk.Frame):
    def __init__(self, parent):
        super().__init__(parent)

        self.canvas = tk.Canvas(self, highlightthickness=0)
        self.scrollbar = ttk.Scrollbar(self, orient="vertical", command=self.canvas.yview)
        self.scrollable_frame = ttk.Frame(self.canvas)

        self.scrollable_frame.bind(
            "<Configure>",
            lambda e: self.canvas.configure(scrollregion=self.canvas.bbox("all"))
        )

        self.canvas.create_window((0, 0), window=self.scrollable_frame, anchor="nw")
        self.canvas.configure(yscrollcommand=self.scrollbar.set)

        self.canvas.pack(side="left", fill="both", expand=True)
        self.scrollbar.pack(side="right", fill="y")

        self.canvas.bind_all("<MouseWheel>", self._on_mousewheel)
        self.canvas.bind_all("<Button-4>", self._on_mousewheel)
        self.canvas.bind_all("<Button-5>", self._on_mousewheel)

    def _on_mousewheel(self, event):
        if event.num == 4:
            delta = -1
        elif event.num == 5:
            delta = 1
        else:
            delta = -1 if event.delta > 0 else 1
        self.canvas.yview_scroll(delta, "units")
''')

add("src/ui/form_toolbar.py", r'''import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk


def add_form_toolbar(parent, commands):
    toolbar = ttk.Frame(parent)
    toolbar.pack(fill=tk.X, pady=(0, 10))

    buttons = [
        ("save_draft", "حفظ المسودة"),
        ("print_pdf", "طباعة / PDF"),
        ("export_excel", "تصدير Excel"),
        ("attach", "إرفاق ملف"),
        ("submit_lock", "تقديم وقفل"),
        ("close", "إغلاق"),
    ]

    for key, label in buttons:
        if key in commands:
            ttk.Button(toolbar, text=label, command=commands[key]).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=4)

    return toolbar
''')


# =========================================================
# acknowledgement dialog
# =========================================================
add("src/ui/acknowledgement_dialog.py", r'''import socket
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk, messagebox
from datetime import datetime

from src.core.app_context import AppContext
from src.core.constants import ACK_TEXT
from src.db.connection import get_connection


class AcknowledgementDialog(tk.Toplevel):
    def __init__(self, parent, ctx):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.ctx = ctx
        self.title("إقرار بمسؤولية دقة البيانات")
        self.geometry("700x450")
        self.transient(parent)
        self.grab_set()
        self._build_ui()

    def _build_ui(self):
        main = ttk.Frame(self, padding=20)
        main.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True)

        ttk.Label(main, text="إقرار بمسؤولية دقة البيانات", font=("Arial", 13, "bold")).pack(anchor="w", pady=(0, 10))

        txt = tk.Text(main, height=12, wrap=tk.WORD, font=("Arial", 10), state=tk.DISABLED, bg="#fff8e1")
        txt.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True, pady=5)
        txt.config(state=tk.NORMAL)
        txt.insert(tk.END, ACK_TEXT)
        txt.config(state=tk.DISABLED)

        self.ack_var = tk.BooleanVar(value=False)
        chk = ttk.Checkbutton(
            main,
            text="لقد اطلعت على الملاحظة وأتعهد بدقة البيانات المدخلة.",
            variable=self.ack_var
        )
        chk.pack(anchor="w", pady=10)

        btns = ttk.Frame(main)
        btns.pack(fill=tk.X)

        ttk.Button(btns, text="متابعة", command=self._confirm).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5)
        ttk.Button(btns, text="خروج", command=self._cancel).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5)

    def _confirm(self):
        if not self.ack_var.get():
            messagebox.showwarning("تنبيه", "يجب تحديد خانة الإقرار للمتابعة.")
            return

        self.ctx.acknowledgement_given = True
        self._save_ack()
        self.destroy()

    def _cancel(self):
        self.ctx.acknowledgement_given = False
        self.destroy()

    def _save_ack(self):
        try:
            conn = get_connection()
            cur = conn.cursor()
            cur.execute("""
                INSERT INTO form_acknowledgments(user_id, school_id, note_version, acknowledged_at, hostname)
                VALUES (?, ?, '2026-v1', ?, ?)
            """, (
                self.ctx.current_user_id,
                self.ctx.selected_school_id,
                datetime.now().isoformat(),
                socket.gethostname()
            ))
            conn.commit()
            conn.close()
        except Exception:
            pass
''')

# =========================================================
# school selection window
# =========================================================
add("src/ui/school_selection_window.py", r'''import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk, messagebox

from src.core.app_context import AppContext
from src.core.messages_ar import msg
from src.core.arabic_utils import normalize_arabic
from src.db.connection import get_connection


class SchoolSelectionWindow(tk.Toplevel):
    def __init__(self, parent, ctx):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.ctx = ctx
        self.title("تحديد المدرسة")
        self.geometry("650x480")
        self.transient(parent)
        self.grab_set()
        self._rows = []
        self._build_ui()

    def _build_ui(self):
        main = ttk.Frame(self, padding=15)
        main.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True)

        ttk.Label(main, text="ابحث بالمدرسة (الاسم أو الرمز الوطني):", font=("Arial", 11, "bold")).pack(anchor="w")

        search_frame = ttk.Frame(main)
        search_frame.pack(fill=tk.X, pady=5)

        self.search_var = tk.StringVar()
        self.search_entry = ttk.Entry(search_frame, textvariable=self.search_var, width=45)
        self.search_entry.pack(side=tk.LEFT, fill=tk.X, expand=True)
        self.search_entry.bind("<KeyRelease>", lambda e: self._search())
        self.search_entry.bind("<Return>", lambda e: self._search())

        ttk.Button(search_frame, text="بحث", command=self._search).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5)

        self.results = tk.Listbox(main, height=14, font=("Arial", 10))
        self.results.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True, pady=5)

        btns = ttk.Frame(main)
        btns.pack(fill=tk.X, pady=5)

        ttk.Button(btns, text="اختيار", command=self._confirm).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5)
        ttk.Button(btns, text="إلغاء", command=self.destroy).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5)

        self._search()

    def _search(self):
        query = self.search_var.get().strip()
        conn = get_connection()
        cur = conn.cursor()

        if query:
            norm = normalize_arabic(query)
            cur.execute("""
                SELECT school_id, school_name, national_code
                FROM schools
                WHERE is_active = 1
                  AND (
                      normalized_name LIKE ?
                      OR school_name LIKE ?
                      OR national_code LIKE ?
                  )
                ORDER BY school_name
                LIMIT 100
            """, (f"%{norm}%", f"%{query}%", f"%{query}%"))
        else:
            cur.execute("""
                SELECT school_id, school_name, national_code
                FROM schools
                WHERE is_active = 1
                ORDER BY school_name
                LIMIT 100
            """)

        self.results.delete(0, tk.END)
        self._rows = cur.fetchall()

        for row in self._rows:
            label = row["school_name"]
            if row["national_code"]:
                label += "  [" + row["national_code"] + "]"
            self.results.insert(tk.END, label)

        if not self._rows:
            self.results.insert(tk.END, "— لا توجد نتائج —")

        conn.close()

    def _confirm(self):
        sel = self.results.curselection()
        if not sel:
            messagebox.showwarning("تنبيه", "اختر مدرسة من القائمة.")
            return

        idx = sel[0]
        if idx >= len(self._rows):
            return

        row = self._rows[idx]
        self.ctx.selected_school_id = row["school_id"]
        self.ctx.selected_school_name = row["school_name"]
        self.destroy()
''')


# =========================================================
# employee lookup panel
# =========================================================
add("src/ui/employee_lookup_panel.py", r'''import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk, messagebox

from src.core.app_context import AppContext
from src.core.validators import detect_login_type
from src.core.messages_ar import msg
from src.db.connection import get_connection
from src.services.employee_auto_fill_service import find_or_create_employee


class EmployeeLookupPanel(ttk.LabelFrame):
    def __init__(self, parent, ctx):
        super().__init__(parent, text="جلب بيانات الموظف تلقائيًا", padding=10)
        self.ctx = ctx

        row1 = ttk.Frame(self)
        row1.pack(fill=tk.X)

        ttk.Label(row1, text="رقم الموظف الوطني أو الوزاري:").pack(side=tk.RIGHT)

        self.number_var = tk.StringVar()
        self.entry = ttk.Entry(row1, textvariable=self.number_var, width=25, justify=tk.CENTER)
        self.entry.pack(side=tk.RIGHT, padx=10)
        self.entry.bind("<Return>", lambda e: self.search())

        ttk.Button(row1, text="بحث تلقائي", command=self.search).pack(side=tk.RIGHT, padx=5)

        self.info_label = ttk.Label(self, text="لم يتم اختيار موظف بعد.", foreground="gray")
        self.info_label.pack(anchor="w", pady=(8, 0))

    def clear(self):
        self.number_var.set("")
        self.ctx.reset_employee()
        self.info_label.config(text="لم يتم اختيار موظف بعد.", foreground="gray")

    def search(self):
        if not self.ctx.require_school():
            messagebox.showwarning("تنبيه", msg("SCHOOL_REQUIRED"))
            return

        value = self.number_var.get().strip()
        login_type = detect_login_type(value)

        if not login_type:
            messagebox.showwarning("تنبيه", msg("EMPLOYEE_NUMBER_INVALID"))
            return

        conn = get_connection()
        employee, error = find_or_create_employee(conn, value, self.ctx.selected_school_id, self.ctx.current_user_id)
        conn.close()

        if not employee:
            self.ctx.reset_employee()
            self.info_label.config(text=msg("EMPLOYEE_NOT_FOUND"), foreground="darkred")
            messagebox.showwarning("تنبيه", msg("EMPLOYEE_NOT_FOUND"))
            return

        self.ctx.selected_employee_id = employee["employee_id"]
        self.ctx.selected_employee_name = employee["full_name"]
        self.ctx.selected_employee_data = employee

        spec = employee.get("specialization_name") or "غير محدد"
        source = employee.get("specialization_source") or "-"
        phone = employee.get("mobile_phone") or "-"
        school = employee.get("school_name") or self.ctx.selected_school_name

        text = (
            "تم العثور على الموظف: " + employee["full_name"] + "\n"
            "الرقم الوطني: " + (employee.get("national_id") or "-") + "\n"
            "الرقم الوزاري: " + (employee.get("ministerial_number") or "-") + "\n"
            "المدرسة: " + school + "\n"
            "التخصص: " + spec + " | المصدر: " + source + "\n"
            "الهاتف: " + phone
        )

        self.info_label.config(text=text, foreground="darkgreen")
        messagebox.showinfo("نجاح", msg("EMPLOYEE_FOUND"))
''')

# =========================================================
# generic form window
# =========================================================
add("src/ui/generic_form_window.py", r'''import json
import os
import shutil
import sqlite3
import tkinter as tk
from datetime import datetime
from tkinter import ttk, messagebox, filedialog

from src.core.app_context import AppContext
from src.core.constants import FORM_ELECTRONIC, COPYRIGHT
from src.core.messages_ar import msg
from src.core.phone_utils import normalize_jordan_mobile, validate_phone_field
from src.db.connection import get_connection, BASE_DIR
from src.services.draft_service import save_draft, load_draft, get_form_id, get_cycle_id
from src.services.export_service import print_form_html, export_excel
from src.services.audit_service import log_audit
from src.ui.form_definitions import FORM_DEFINITIONS
from src.ui.scrollable_frame import ScrollableFrame
from src.ui.form_toolbar import add_form_toolbar
from src.ui.searchable_combo import SearchableCombobox


class GenericFormWindow(tk.Toplevel):
    def __init__(self, parent, ctx, form_code):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.ctx = ctx
        self.form_code = form_code
        self.defn = FORM_DEFINITIONS[form_code]
        self.title(self.defn["title"])

        sw = self.winfo_screenwidth()
        sh = self.winfo_screenheight()
        w = int(sw * 0.78)
        h = int(sh * 0.82)
        w = max(w, 850)
        h = max(h, 650)
        x = (sw - w) // 2
        y = (sh - h) // 2
        self.geometry("{0}x{1}+{2}+{3}".format(w, h, x, y))
        self.minsize(850, 650)

        self.loading = True
        self.locked = False
        self.submission_id = None
        self.vars = {}
        self.widgets = {}
        self.input_widgets = []
        self.autosave_after_id = None

        self._build_ui()
        self._load_data()
        self.loading = False

    def _build_ui(self):
        main = ttk.Frame(self, padding=12)
        main.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True)

        info = ttk.Frame(main)
        info.pack(fill=tk.X, pady=(0, 8))

        ttk.Label(info, text="المدرسة: " + (self.ctx.selected_school_name or "-"), font=("Arial", 10, "bold")).pack(side=tk.RIGHT)
        ttk.Label(info, text="الموظف: " + (self.ctx.selected_employee_name or "-")).pack(side=tk.RIGHT, padx=15)
        ttk.Label(info, text="المستخدم: " + (self.ctx.current_user_name or "-")).pack(side=tk.LEFT)

        commands = {
            "save_draft": self._save_draft_action,
            "print_pdf": self._print_action,
            "export_excel": self._export_action,
            "attach": self._attach_action,
            "submit_lock": self._submit_action,
            "close": self.destroy,
        }
        add_form_toolbar(main, commands)

        self.scroll = ScrollableFrame(main)
        self.scroll.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True)

        form = self.scroll.scrollable_frame

        for field in self.defn["fields"]:
            row = ttk.Frame(form)
            row.pack(fill=tk.X, pady=6)

            label_text = field["label"]
            if field.get("required"):
                label_text += " *"

            ttk.Label(row, text=label_text, width=38, anchor="w").pack(side=tk.RIGHT)

            ftype = field["type"]
            name = field["name"]

            if ftype == "text":
                var = tk.StringVar()
                entry = ttk.Entry(row, textvariable=var, width=45)
                entry.pack(side=tk.RIGHT, padx=5)
                entry.bind("<KeyRelease>", self._on_change)
                self.vars[name] = var
                self.widgets[name] = entry
                self.input_widgets.append(entry)

            elif ftype == "number":
                var = tk.StringVar()
                entry = ttk.Entry(row, textvariable=var, width=20, justify=tk.CENTER)
                entry.pack(side=tk.RIGHT, padx=5)
                entry.bind("<KeyRelease>", self._on_change)
                self.vars[name] = var
                self.widgets[name] = entry
                self.input_widgets.append(entry)

            elif ftype == "phone":
                var = tk.StringVar()
                entry = ttk.Entry(row, textvariable=var, width=20, justify=tk.CENTER)
                entry.pack(side=tk.RIGHT, padx=5)
                entry.bind("<KeyRelease>", self._on_change)
                self.vars[name] = var
                self.widgets[name] = entry
                self.input_widgets.append(entry)

            elif ftype == "checkbox":
                var = tk.BooleanVar(value=False)
                chk = ttk.Checkbutton(row, variable=var, command=self._on_change)
                chk.pack(side=tk.RIGHT, padx=5)
                self.vars[name] = var
                self.widgets[name] = chk
                self.input_widgets.append(chk)

            elif ftype == "choice":
                var = tk.StringVar()
                cb = ttk.Combobox(row, textvariable=var, values=field.get("options", []), width=35, state="readonly")
                cb.pack(side=tk.RIGHT, padx=5)
                cb.bind("<<ComboboxSelected>>", self._on_change)
                self.vars[name] = var
                self.widgets[name] = cb
                self.input_widgets.append(cb)

            elif ftype == "subject":
                combo = SearchableCombobox(row, choices=self._load_subject_choices(), width=45, command=lambda i, d: self._on_change())
                combo.pack(side=tk.RIGHT, padx=5)
                self.vars[name] = None
                self.widgets[name] = combo
                self.input_widgets.append(combo)

            elif ftype == "textarea":
                txt = tk.Text(row, height=4, width=55, wrap=tk.WORD)
                txt.pack(side=tk.RIGHT, padx=5)
                txt.bind("<KeyRelease>", self._on_change)
                self.vars[name] = None
                self.widgets[name] = txt
                self.input_widgets.append(txt)

        ttk.Label(main, text=COPYRIGHT, anchor=tk.CENTER).pack(side=tk.BOTTOM, fill=tk.X, pady=(8, 0))

    def _load_subject_choices(self):
        conn = get_connection()
        cur = conn.cursor()
        cur.execute("""
            SELECT subject_id, canonical_name_pdf
            FROM subjects
            WHERE is_active = 1
              AND COALESCE(status, 'ACTIVE') = 'ACTIVE'
            ORDER BY is_pdf_canonical DESC, canonical_name_pdf
        """)
        choices = [(row["subject_id"], row["canonical_name_pdf"]) for row in cur.fetchall()]
        conn.close()
        return choices

    def _load_data(self):
        conn = get_connection()
        form_id = get_form_id(conn, self.form_code)
        cycle_id = get_cycle_id(conn)

        if form_id and cycle_id and self.ctx.selected_employee_id:
            cur = conn.cursor()
            cur.execute("""
                SELECT submission_id, is_locked, form_data
                FROM submissions
                WHERE employee_id = ? AND form_id = ? AND cycle_id = ?
            """, (self.ctx.selected_employee_id, form_id, cycle_id))
            row = cur.fetchone()
            if row:
                self.submission_id = row["submission_id"]
                self.locked = bool(row["is_locked"])
                try:
                    data = json.loads(row["form_data"] or "{}")
                except Exception:
                    data = {}
                self._apply_data(data)
                if self.locked:
                    self._set_locked_ui()
                conn.close()
                return

        draft = load_draft(conn, self.form_code, self.ctx)
        conn.close()

        if draft:
            self._apply_data(draft)
        else:
            self._apply_employee_defaults()

    def _apply_employee_defaults(self):
        emp = self.ctx.selected_employee_data or {}
        if "mobile_phone" in self.vars:
            self.vars["mobile_phone"].set(emp.get("mobile_phone") or "")

        if "specialization" in self.widgets and emp.get("specialization_id"):
            self.widgets["specialization"].set_selection(emp.get("specialization_id"), emp.get("specialization_name"))

        if "years_experience" in self.vars:
            self.vars["years_experience"].set(str(emp.get("years_experience") or ""))

        if "grade" in self.vars:
            self.vars["grade"].set(emp.get("grade") or "")

        if "residence" in self.vars:
            self.vars["residence"].set(emp.get("residence") or "")

        if "university_name" in self.vars:
            self.vars["university_name"].set(emp.get("university_name") or "")

    def _apply_data(self, data):
        for field in self.defn["fields"]:
            name = field["name"]
            value = data.get(name)
            ftype = field["type"]

            if value is None:
                continue

            if ftype in ("text", "number", "phone", "choice"):
                self.vars[name].set(str(value))
            elif ftype == "checkbox":
                self.vars[name].set(bool(value))
            elif ftype == "subject" and isinstance(value, dict):
                self.widgets[name].set_selection(value.get("id"), value.get("display"))
            elif ftype == "textarea":
                widget = self.widgets[name]
                widget.delete("1.0", tk.END)
                widget.insert("1.0", str(value))

    def _collect_data(self):
        data = {}
        for field in self.defn["fields"]:
            name = field["name"]
            ftype = field["type"]

            if ftype in ("text", "number", "choice"):
                data[name] = self.vars[name].get().strip()
            elif ftype == "phone":
                data[name] = normalize_jordan_mobile(self.vars[name].get())
            elif ftype == "checkbox":
                data[name] = bool(self.vars[name].get())
            elif ftype == "subject":
                combo = self.widgets[name]
                data[name] = {"id": combo.get_selected_id(), "display": combo.get_text()}
            elif ftype == "textarea":
                data[name] = self.widgets[name].get("1.0", tk.END).strip()

        data["school_id"] = self.ctx.selected_school_id
        data["employee_id"] = self.ctx.selected_employee_id
        data["acknowledgement"] = self.ctx.acknowledgement_given
        return data

    def _validate(self, data):
        errors = []

        for field in self.defn["fields"]:
            if not field.get("required"):
                continue

            name = field["name"]
            value = data.get(name)

            if field["type"] == "subject":
                if not isinstance(value, dict) or not value.get("id"):
                    errors.append(field["label"] + " مطلوب.")
            elif field["type"] == "phone":
                ok, message = validate_phone_field(value or "")
                if not ok:
                    errors.append(message)
            elif field["type"] == "checkbox":
                pass
            else:
                if value is None or str(value).strip() == "":
                    errors.append(field["label"] + " مطلوب.")

        if self.form_code == "QF71-4-6":
            if data.get("receives_official_car") and data.get("willing_to_transport_with_private_car"):
                errors.append("لا يجوز استخدام سيارة خاصة لمن يتقاضى سيارة رسمية.")
            if data.get("willing_to_transport_with_private_car") and not data.get("owns_private_car"):
                errors.append("لا يمكن طلب النقل بسيارة خاصة دون امتلاك سيارة.")
            if data.get("willing_to_transport_with_private_car") and not data.get("car_registered_in_owner_name"):
                errors.append("لا يجوز اعتماد السيارة الخاصة إلا إذا كانت مسجلة باسم الموظف.")

        return errors

    def _on_change(self, event=None):
        if self.loading or self.locked:
            return
        if self.autosave_after_id:
            self.after_cancel(self.autosave_after_id)
        self.autosave_after_id = self.after(800, self._autosave)

    def _autosave(self):
        if self.loading or self.locked:
            return
        data = self._collect_data()
        conn = get_connection()
        save_draft(conn, self.form_code, self.ctx, data)
        conn.close()

    def _save_draft_action(self):
        data = self._collect_data()
        conn = get_connection()
        save_draft(conn, self.form_code, self.ctx, data)
        conn.close()
        messagebox.showinfo("تم", msg("DRAFT_SAVED"))

    def _print_action(self):
        data = self._collect_data()
        path = print_form_html(self.form_code, self.defn["title"], data, self.ctx, self.locked)
        messagebox.showinfo("طباعة", msg("PRINT_SUCCESS") + "\n" + path)

    def _export_action(self):
        data = self._collect_data()
        errors = self._validate(data)
        protected = self.form_code == FORM_ELECTRONIC

        if errors and not self.locked:
            if not messagebox.askyesno("تحذير", msg("EXPORT_DRAFT_WARNING") + "\n\n" + "\n".join(errors[:5])):
                return

        path = export_excel(self.form_code, self.defn["title"], data, self.ctx, protected=protected)
        if protected:
            messagebox.showinfo("تصدير", msg("EXPORT_PROTECTED_EXCEL") + "\n" + path)
        else:
            messagebox.showinfo("تصدير", msg("EXPORT_SUCCESS") + "\n" + path)

    def _attach_action(self):
        file_path = filedialog.askopenfilename(title="اختر ملفًا للإرفاق")
        if not file_path:
            return

        uploads = BASE_DIR / "uploads" / "attachments"
        uploads.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

        file_name = os.path.basename(file_path)
        dest = uploads / ("{0}_{1}".format(self.ctx.selected_employee_id or 0, file_name))
        shutil.copy2(file_path, dest)

        conn = get_connection()
        cur = conn.cursor()
        cur.execute("""
            INSERT INTO attachments(submission_id, employee_id, file_name, original_file_name, file_path, category, uploaded_by)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (
            self.submission_id,
            self.ctx.selected_employee_id,
            dest.name,
            file_name,
            str(dest),
            "new_employee_paper",
            self.ctx.current_user_id
        ))
        conn.commit()
        conn.close()

        messagebox.showinfo("إرفاق", msg("ATTACHMENT_UPLOADED" if "ATTACHMENT_UPLOADED" in __import__("src.core.messages_ar", fromlist=["MESSAGES_AR"]).MESSAGES_AR else "تم إرفاق الملف بنجاح."))

    def _submit_action(self):
        if self.locked:
            messagebox.showwarning("تنبيه", msg("LOCKED_RECORD"))
            return

        if not self.ctx.acknowledgement_given:
            messagebox.showwarning("تنبيه", msg("ACK_REQUIRED"))
            return

        data = self._collect_data()
        errors = self._validate(data)

        if errors:
            messagebox.showerror("خطأ", msg("SUBMIT_INCOMPLETE") + "\n\n" + "\n".join(errors))
            return

        if not messagebox.askyesno("تأكيد", msg("SUBMIT_CONFIRM")):
            return

        conn = get_connection()
        form_id = get_form_id(conn, self.form_code)
        cycle_id = get_cycle_id(conn)
        payload = json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False)
        now = datetime.now().isoformat()

        cur = conn.cursor()

        if self.submission_id:
            cur.execute("""
                UPDATE submissions
                SET status = 4,
                    is_locked = 1,
                    locked_at = ?,
                    locked_by = ?,
                    form_data = ?,
                    updated_at = ?
                WHERE submission_id = ?
            """, (now, self.ctx.current_user_id, payload, now, self.submission_id))
        else:
            cur.execute("""
                INSERT INTO submissions(
                    form_id, employee_id, cycle_id, school_id,
                    status, is_locked, locked_at, locked_by,
                    entered_by, entered_at, form_data
                )
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 4, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (
                form_id,
                self.ctx.selected_employee_id,
                cycle_id,
                self.ctx.selected_school_id,
                now,
                self.ctx.current_user_id,
                self.ctx.current_user_id,
                now,
                payload
            ))
            self.submission_id = cur.lastrowid

        conn.commit()
        log_audit(conn, "submissions", self.submission_id, "SUBMIT_AND_LOCK", new_values=payload, user_id=self.ctx.current_user_id)
        conn.close()

        self.locked = True
        self._set_locked_ui()
        messagebox.showinfo("نجاح", msg("SUBMIT_SUCCESS"))

    def _set_locked_ui(self):
        for widget in self.input_widgets:
            try:
                if isinstance(widget, SearchableCombobox):
                    widget.set_state(tk.DISABLED)
                elif isinstance(widget, tk.Text):
                    widget.config(state=tk.DISABLED)
                elif isinstance(widget, ttk.Combobox):
                    widget.config(state=tk.DISABLED)
                else:
                    widget.config(state=tk.DISABLED)
            except Exception:
                pass

        ttk.Label(self, text="هذا السجل مقدم ومقفول ولا يمكن تعديله.", foreground="darkred", font=("Arial", 10, "bold")).pack(fill=tk.X, pady=5)
''')

add("src/ui/main_window.py", r'''import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk, messagebox
import webbrowser

from src.core.constants import APP_TITLE, COPYRIGHT, ORG_NAME, DEPT_NAME, FORM_ELECTRONIC, FORM_MANAGERS, FORM_UNIVERSITIES
from src.core.messages_ar import msg
from src.core.app_context import AppContext


class MainWindow:
    def __init__(self, ctx):
        self.ctx = ctx
        self.root = tk.Tk()
        self._build_ui()

    def run(self):
        self.root.mainloop()

    def _build_ui(self):
        self.root.title(APP_TITLE)

        sw = self.root.winfo_screenwidth()
        sh = self.root.winfo_screenheight()
        w = int(sw * 0.85)
        h = int(sh * 0.85)
        w = max(w, 980)
        h = max(h, 700)
        x = (sw - w) // 2
        y = (sh - h) // 2
        self.root.geometry("{0}x{1}+{2}+{3}".format(w, h, x, y))
        self.root.minsize(980, 700)

        menu = tk.Menu(self.root)

        file_menu = tk.Menu(menu, tearoff=0)
        file_menu.add_command(label="خروج", command=self._exit)
        menu.add_cascade(label="ملف", menu=file_menu)

        forms_menu = tk.Menu(menu, tearoff=0)
        forms_menu.add_command(label="المراقبة الإلكترونية", command=lambda: self._open_form(FORM_ELECTRONIC))
        forms_menu.add_command(label="مراقبة المدراء QF71-4-6", command=lambda: self._open_form(FORM_MANAGERS))
        forms_menu.add_command(label="الجامعات QF71-4-9", command=lambda: self._open_form(FORM_UNIVERSITIES))
        menu.add_cascade(label="النماذج", menu=forms_menu)

        admin_menu = tk.Menu(menu, tearoff=0)
        admin_menu.add_command(label="إدارة المستخدمين", command=self._coming_soon)
        admin_menu.add_command(label="إدارة الأذونات", command=self._coming_soon)
        admin_menu.add_command(label="إدارة المدارس", command=self._coming_soon)
        admin_menu.add_command(label="سجل التدقيق", command=self._coming_soon)
        menu.add_cascade(label="الإدارة", menu=admin_menu)

        help_menu = tk.Menu(menu, tearoff=0)
        help_menu.add_command(label="المساعدة والتواصل", command=self._open_help)
        help_menu.add_command(label="رابط التوثيق", command=self._open_doc_url)
        help_menu.add_command(label="رابط الذكاء الاصطناعي", command=self._open_ai_url)
        menu.add_cascade(label="المساعدة", menu=help_menu)

        self.root.config(menu=menu)

        header = ttk.Frame(self.root, padding=10)
        header.pack(fill=tk.X)

        ttk.Label(header, text=ORG_NAME + " — " + DEPT_NAME, font=("Arial", 11, "bold")).pack(side=tk.RIGHT)
        ttk.Label(header, text="المستخدم: " + self.ctx.current_user_name + " | الدور: " + self.ctx.current_role).pack(side=tk.LEFT)

        self.school_bar = ttk.Frame(self.root, padding=5)
        self.school_bar.pack(fill=tk.X)

        self.school_label = ttk.Label(self.school_bar, text="المدرسة: لم تٌحدد بعد", font=("Arial", 10, "bold"))
        self.school_label.pack(side=tk.RIGHT)

        ttk.Button(self.school_bar, text="تحديد / تغيير المدرسة", command=self._select_school).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5)

        ack_frame = ttk.LabelFrame(self.root, text="تنبيه هام", padding=8)
        ack_frame.pack(fill=tk.X, padx=10, pady=5)

        ttk.Label(
            ack_frame,
            text="هذا العمل يتطلب أمانة ودقة في البيانات. مٌدخل البيانات ومٌدير المدرسة يتحملان المسؤولية الكاملة.",
            wraplength=900,
            justify=tk.RIGHT,
            foreground="darkred"
        ).pack()

        from src.ui.employee_lookup_panel import EmployeeLookupPanel
        self.employee_panel = EmployeeLookupPanel(self.root, self.ctx)
        self.employee_panel.pack(fill=tk.X, padx=10, pady=5)

        self.content = ttk.Frame(self.root, padding=10)
        self.content.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True)

        ttk.Label(
            self.content,
            text="1) حدد المدرسة أولًا.\n2) أدخل رقم الموظف الوطني أو الوزاري لجلب البيانات تلقائيًا.\n3) ثم افتح النموذج المطلوب من قائمة النماذج.",
            font=("Arial", 12),
            justify=tk.RIGHT
        ).pack(expand=True)

        ttk.Label(self.root, text=COPYRIGHT, anchor=tk.CENTER, relief=tk.RIDGE, padding=4).pack(side=tk.BOTTOM, fill=tk.X)

    def _select_school(self):
        from src.ui.school_selection_window import SchoolSelectionWindow
        win = SchoolSelectionWindow(self.root, self.ctx)
        self.root.wait_window(win)
        if self.ctx.selected_school_id:
            self.school_label.config(text="المدرسة: " + self.ctx.selected_school_name)
            self.employee_panel.clear()
            messagebox.showinfo("تم", msg("SCHOOL_SELECTED", self.ctx.selected_school_name))

    def _open_form(self, form_code):
        if not self.ctx.require_school():
            messagebox.showwarning("تنبيه", msg("SCHOOL_REQUIRED"))
            self._select_school()
            if not self.ctx.require_school():
                return

        if not self.ctx.require_employee():
            messagebox.showwarning("تنبيه", "يجب إدخال رقم الموظف وجلب بياناته أولًا.")
            return

        from src.ui.acknowledgement_dialog import AcknowledgementDialog
        ack = AcknowledgementDialog(self.root, self.ctx)
        self.root.wait_window(ack)

        if not self.ctx.acknowledgement_given:
            messagebox.showwarning("تنبيه", msg("ACK_REQUIRED"))
            return

        from src.ui.generic_form_window import GenericFormWindow
        GenericFormWindow(self.root, self.ctx, form_code)

    def _coming_soon(self):
        messagebox.showinfo("قريبًا", "هذه الشاشة مخصصة للمرحلة التالية من التطوير.")

    def _open_help(self):
        from src.ui.help_window import HelpWindow
        HelpWindow(self.root)

    def _open_doc_url(self):
        from src.core.constants import DOC_URL
        webbrowser.open(DOC_URL)

    def _open_ai_url(self):
        from src.core.constants import AI_URL
        webbrowser.open(AI_URL)

    def _exit(self):
        if messagebox.askyesno("خروج", "هل تريد الخروج؟"):
            self.root.destroy()
''')


# =========================================================
# login window
# =========================================================
add("src/ui/login_window.py", r'''import socket
import getpass
import tkinter as tk
from tkinter import ttk, messagebox
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

from src.core.constants import (
    APP_TITLE, COPYRIGHT, MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS,
    MINISTERIAL_LENGTH, NATIONAL_LENGTH, ORG_NAME, DEPT_NAME
)
from src.core.validators import detect_login_type
from src.core.messages_ar import msg
from src.db.connection import get_connection


class LoginApp:
    def __init__(self):
        self.root = tk.Tk()
        self.attempts = 0
        self.conn = None
        self._build_ui()

    def run(self):
        self.root.mainloop()

    def _build_ui(self):
        self.root.title(APP_TITLE)
        self.root.resizable(False, False)

        sw = self.root.winfo_screenwidth()
        sh = self.root.winfo_screenheight()
        w = min(560, int(sw * 0.9))
        h = min(420, int(sh * 0.85))
        x = (sw - w) // 2
        y = (sh - h) // 2
        self.root.geometry("{0}x{1}+{2}+{3}".format(w, h, x, y))

        style = ttk.Style()
        style.configure("Title.TLabel", font=("Arial", 14, "bold"))
        style.configure("Sub.TLabel", font=("Arial", 10))
        style.configure("TButton", font=("Arial", 10))
        style.configure("TEntry", font=("Arial", 11))

        main = ttk.Frame(self.root, padding=20)
        main.pack(fill=tk.BOTH, expand=True)

        ttk.Label(
            main,
            text=ORG_NAME + "\n" + DEPT_NAME,
            style="Sub.TLabel",
            justify=tk.CENTER
        ).pack(pady=(0, 5))

        ttk.Label(
            main,
            text=APP_TITLE,
            style="Title.TLabel",
            justify=tk.CENTER
        ).pack(pady=(0, 20))

        form = ttk.Frame(main)
        form.pack(pady=10)

        ttk.Label(form, text="رقم الدخول:").grid(row=0, column=0, sticky="e", padx=5, pady=8)

        self.login_var = tk.StringVar()
        self.login_entry = ttk.Entry(form, textvariable=self.login_var, width=28, justify=tk.CENTER)
        self.login_entry.grid(row=0, column=1, padx=5, pady=8)
        self.login_entry.focus_set()

        ttk.Label(
            form,
            text="أدخل الرقم الوزاري ({0} أرقام) أو الرقم الوطني ({1} أرقام)".format(MINISTERIAL_LENGTH, NATIONAL_LENGTH),
            style="Sub.TLabel"
        ).grid(row=1, column=0, columnspan=2, pady=(0, 10))

        buttons = ttk.Frame(main)
        buttons.pack(pady=10)

        ttk.Button(buttons, text="دخول", command=self._attempt_login).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5)
        ttk.Button(buttons, text="خروج", command=self._confirm_exit).pack(side=tk.LEFT, padx=5)

        ttk.Label(main, text=COPYRIGHT, style="Sub.TLabel").pack(side=tk.BOTTOM, pady=5)

        self.root.bind("<Return>", lambda e: self._attempt_login())
        self.root.protocol("WM_DELETE_WINDOW", self._confirm_exit)

    def _attempt_login(self):
        value = self.login_var.get().strip()
        login_type = detect_login_type(value)

        self.conn = get_connection()

        if not login_type:
            self.attempts += 1
            self._log_attempt(value, None, False, "مدخل غير صالح")

            if self.attempts >= MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS:
                messagebox.showerror("إنهاء الجلسة", msg("LOGIN_EXIT"))
                self.conn.close()
                self.root.destroy()
                return

            messagebox.showwarning(
                "محاولة غير صالحة",
                msg("LOGIN_INVALID_FORMAT") + "\n" + msg("LOGIN_ATTEMPTS_LEFT", MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS - self.attempts)
            )
            self.conn.close()
            return

        user_row, message = self._validate_user(value, login_type)

        if user_row:
            self._reset_attempts(user_row["user_id"])
            self._log_attempt(value, login_type, True, "نجاح")
            self.conn.close()
            messagebox.showinfo("نجاح", msg("LOGIN_SUCCESS") + "\n" + user_row["full_name"])
            self.root.destroy()
            self._open_main(user_row)
            return

        self.attempts += 1
        self._register_failed(value, login_type, message)
        self._log_attempt(value, login_type, False, message)

        if self.attempts >= MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS:
            messagebox.showerror("إنهاء الجلسة", msg("LOGIN_EXIT"))
            self.conn.close()
            self.root.destroy()
            return

        messagebox.showwarning(
            "فشل الدخول",
            message + "\n" + msg("LOGIN_ATTEMPTS_LEFT", MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS - self.attempts)
        )
        self.conn.close()

    def _validate_user(self, value, login_type):
        cur = self.conn.cursor()
        col = "ministerial_number" if login_type == "MINISTERIAL" else "national_id"
        cur.execute("""
            SELECT u.user_id,
                   u.full_name,
                   COALESCE(r.role_name, 'مستخدم') AS role,
                   u.is_active,
                   u.failed_login_attempts,
                   u.locked_until
            FROM users u
            LEFT JOIN roles r ON r.role_id = u.role_id
            WHERE u.{0} = ?
        """.format(col), (value,))
        row = cur.fetchone()

        if not row:
            return None, msg("LOGIN_FAILED")
        if not row["is_active"]:
            return None, "الحساب غير مفعّل."
        if row["locked_until"]:
            try:
                lu = datetime.fromisoformat(row["locked_until"])
                if datetime.now() < lu:
                    return None, "الحساب مقفل مؤقتًا."
            except Exception:
                pass
        return row, "OK"

    def _register_failed(self, value, login_type, message):
        cur = self.conn.cursor()
        col = "ministerial_number" if login_type == "MINISTERIAL" else "national_id"
        cur.execute("SELECT user_id, failed_login_attempts FROM users WHERE {0} = ?".format(col), (value,))
        row = cur.fetchone()
        if row:
            attempts = row["failed_login_attempts"] + 1
            locked = None
            if attempts >= MAX_LOGIN_ATTEMPTS:
                locked = (datetime.now() + timedelta(minutes=15)).isoformat()
            cur.execute("""
                UPDATE users SET failed_login_attempts = ?, locked_until = ? WHERE user_id = ?
            """, (attempts, locked, row["user_id"]))
            self.conn.commit()

    def _reset_attempts(self, user_id):
        cur = self.conn.cursor()
        cur.execute("""
            UPDATE users SET failed_login_attempts = 0, locked_until = NULL, last_login_at = ?
            WHERE user_id = ?
        """, (datetime.now().isoformat(), user_id))
        self.conn.commit()

    def _log_attempt(self, value, ltype, success, message):
        cur = self.conn.cursor()
        cur.execute("""
            INSERT INTO login_attempts(login_value, detected_type, success, message, hostname, machine_user)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (value, ltype, 1 if success else 0, message, socket.gethostname(), getpass.getuser()))
        self.conn.commit()

    def _confirm_exit(self):
        if messagebox.askyesno("خروج", "هل تريد الخروج من النظام؟"):
            self.root.destroy()

    def _open_main(self, user_row):
        from src.ui.main_window import MainWindow
        from src.core.app_context import AppContext

        ctx = AppContext()
        ctx.current_user_id = user_row["user_id"]
        ctx.current_user_name = user_row["full_name"]
        ctx.current_role = user_row["role"]

        app = MainWindow(ctx)
        app.run()
''')


# =========================================================
# main entry
# =========================================================
add("src/main.py", r'''import sys
import os

if getattr(sys, "frozen", False):
    BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(sys.executable)
else:
    BASE_DIR = os.path.abspath(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".."))

if BASE_DIR not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, BASE_DIR)

from src.ui.login_window import LoginApp


def main():
    if sys.maxsize > 2**32:
        print("Warning: Running on 64-bit Python. For 32-bit build, use Python x86.")
    app = LoginApp()
    app.run()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
''')


# =========================================================
# init db + seed
# =========================================================
add("scripts/init_db.py", r'''import json
import sqlite3
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(BASE_DIR) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(BASE_DIR))

from src.core.arabic_utils import normalize_arabic


DB_PATH = BASE_DIR / "exam2026.db"
SCHEMA_PATH = BASE_DIR / "src" / "db" / "schema.sql"


PDF_SUBJECTS = [

PERMISSIONS = [
    ("can_login", "تسجيل الدخول", "السماح بدخول النظام", "auth"),
    ("can_view_dashboard", "عرض لوحة التحكم", "الوصول للشاشة الرئيسية", "dashboard"),
    ("can_manage_users", "إدارة المستخدمين", "إضافة وتعديل المستخدمين", "users"),

    ("can_add_permission_manually", "إضافة إذن يدويًا", "السماح بإضافة صلاحية عند عدم العثور", "permissions"),

    ("can_view_audit_logs", "عرض سجل التدقيق", "مراجعة الأحداث", "security"),
]


ROLE_PERMISSIONS = {
    "ADMIN": [p[0] for p in PERMISSIONS],
    "EXAM_MANAGER": [
        "can_login", "can_view_dashboard", "can_enter_electronic_form",
        "can_enter_manager_form", "can_enter_university_form",
        "can_approve_submission", "can_print_form", "can_export_pdf",
        "can_export_protected_excel", "can_view_audit_logs"
    ],
    "SCHOOL_PRINCIPAL": [
        "can_login", "can_view_dashboard", "can_enter_electronic_form",
        "can_enter_manager_form", "can_enter_university_form",
        "can_print_form", "can_export_pdf"
    ],
    "DATA_ENTRY": [
        "can_login", "can_view_dashboard", "can_enter_electronic_form",
        "can_enter_manager_form", "can_enter_university_form"
    ],
}


def get_conn():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(str(DB_PATH))
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON")
    return conn


def seed(conn):
    cur = conn.cursor()

    cur.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO roles(role_code, role_name, description) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                ("ADMIN", "مدير النظام", "صلاحيات كاملة"))
    cur.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO roles(role_code, role_name, description) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                ("EXAM_MANAGER", "مسؤول الامتحانات", "اعتماد وتقارير"))
    cur.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO roles(role_code, role_name, description) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                ("SCHOOL_PRINCIPAL", "مدير مدرسة", "إدخال واعتماد مدرسته"))
    cur.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO roles(role_code, role_name, description) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                ("DATA_ENTRY", "مدخل بيانات", "إدخال فقط"))

    for code, name, desc, module in PERMISSIONS:
        cur.execute("""
            INSERT OR IGNORE INTO permissions(permission_code, permission_name, description, module, is_active, is_manual, status)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 1, 0, 'ACTIVE')
        """, (code, name, desc, module))

    for role_code, perm_codes in ROLE_PERMISSIONS.items():
        cur.execute("SELECT role_id FROM roles WHERE role_code = ?", (role_code,))
        role_row = cur.fetchone()
        if not role_row:
            continue
        role_id = role_row["role_id"]

        for perm_code in perm_codes:
            cur.execute("SELECT permission_id FROM permissions WHERE permission_code = ?", (perm_code,))
            perm_row = cur.fetchone()
            if not perm_row:
                continue
            cur.execute("""
                INSERT OR IGNORE INTO role_permissions(role_id, permission_id)
                VALUES (?, ?)
            """, (role_id, perm_row["permission_id"]))

    cur.execute("SELECT role_id FROM roles WHERE role_code = 'ADMIN'")
    admin_role_id = cur.fetchone()["role_id"]

    cur.execute("""
        INSERT OR IGNORE INTO users(full_name, national_id, ministerial_number, role_id, is_active, approval_status)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    """, ("مستخدم تجريبي", "9881018940", "172571", admin_role_id, 1, "APPROVED"))

    cur.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO directorates(directorate_name) VALUES (?)",
                ("مديرية التربية والتعليم",))
    cur.execute("SELECT directorate_id FROM directorates WHERE directorate_name = ?",
                ("مديرية التربية والتعليم",))
    directorate_id = cur.fetchone()["directorate_id"]

    schools = [
        ("مدرسة تجريبية", "100001"),
        ("مدرسة الخوارزمي", "100002"),
        ("مدرسة ابن سينا", "100003"),
    ]

    for school_name, national_code in schools:
        normalized = normalize_arabic(school_name)
        cur.execute("""
            INSERT OR IGNORE INTO schools(school_name, normalized_name, national_code, directorate_id, phone, address)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (school_name, normalized, national_code, directorate_id, "0771234567", "عمان"))

    cur.execute("SELECT school_id FROM schools WHERE school_name = ?", ("مدرسة تجريبية",))
    school_id = cur.fetchone()["school_id"]

    cur.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO forms(form_code, form_name, revision, effective_year) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                ("ELECTRONIC", "المراقبة الإلكترونية", "2026", 2026))
    cur.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO forms(form_code, form_name, revision, effective_year) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                ("QF71-4-6", "مدير ومديرات المدارس ورؤساء الأقسام والمشرفين", "Rev.b", 2026))
    cur.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO forms(form_code, form_name, revision, effective_year) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                ("QF71-4-9", "أسماء الموظفين والمعلمين والمعلمات الحاصلين على شهادات جامعية", "Rev.b", 2026))

    cur.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO exam_cycles(cycle_name, year, exam_type) VALUES (?, ?, ?)",
                ("الامتحان التكميلي", 2026, "تكميلي"))

    for canonical, en, category, aliases in PDF_SUBJECTS:
        normalized = normalize_arabic(canonical)
        cur.execute("""
            INSERT OR IGNORE INTO subjects(canonical_name_pdf, name_en, category, normalized_key, is_active, source, is_pdf_canonical, status)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 1, 'PDF', 1, 'ACTIVE')
        """, (canonical, en, category, normalized))

        cur.execute("SELECT subject_id FROM subjects WHERE canonical_name_pdf = ?", (canonical,))
        subject_id = cur.fetchone()["subject_id"]

        cur.execute("""
            INSERT OR IGNORE INTO subject_aliases(subject_id, alias_text, normalized_alias, source)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, 'pdf')
        """, (subject_id, canonical, normalized))

        for alias in aliases:
            normalized_alias = normalize_arabic(alias)
            if not normalized_alias:
                continue
            cur.execute("""
                INSERT OR IGNORE INTO subject_aliases(subject_id, alias_text, normalized_alias, source)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, 'manual')
            """, (subject_id, alias, normalized_alias))

    other_norm = normalize_arabic("أخرى")
    cur.execute("""
        INSERT OR IGNORE INTO subjects(canonical_name_pdf, name_en, category, normalized_key, is_active, source, is_pdf_canonical, status)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 1, 'EXCEL_EMPTY', 0, 'ACTIVE')
    """, ("أخرى", "Other", "افتراضي - قيمة Excel فارغة", other_norm))

    cur.execute("SELECT subject_id FROM subjects WHERE canonical_name_pdf = ?", ("أخرى",))
    other_subject_id = cur.fetchone()["subject_id"]

    cur.execute("""
        INSERT OR IGNORE INTO employees(national_id, ministerial_number, full_name, school_id, specialization_id, mobile_phone, job_title)
        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
    """, ("1234567890", "654321", "موظف تجريبي", school_id, other_subject_id, "0771234567", "معلم"))

    conn.commit()


def init():
    conn = get_conn()
    conn.executescript(SCHEMA_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    seed(conn)
    conn.close()

    print("Database initialized:", DB_PATH)
    print("Login demo:")
    print("Ministerial: 172571")
    print("National: 9881018940")
    print("Employee demo national: 1234567890")
    print("Employee demo ministerial: 654321")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    init()
''')


# =========================================================
# import excel employees
# =========================================================
add("scripts/import_excel_employees.py", r'''import argparse
import json
import sqlite3
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
if str(BASE_DIR) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(BASE_DIR))

from openpyxl import load_workbook

from src.core.arabic_utils import normalize_arabic
from src.core.phone_utils import normalize_jordan_mobile
from src.core.subject_resolver import SubjectResolver
from src.db.connection import get_connection


HEADER_MAP = {
    "الرقم الوطني": "national_id",
    "national_id": "national_id",
    "الرقم الوزاري": "ministerial_number",
    "ministerial_number": "ministerial_number",
    "الاسم": "full_name",
    "اسم الموظف": "full_name",
    "المدرسة": "school_name",
    "school_name": "school_name",
    "رمز المدرسة": "school_national_code",
    "التخصص": "specialization_raw",
    "specialization": "specialization_raw",
    "الهاتف": "mobile_phone_raw",
    "رقم الهاتف": "mobile_phone_raw",
    "mobile_phone": "mobile_phone_raw",
    "سنوات الخبرة": "years_experience",
    "الدرجة": "grade",
    "مكان السكن": "residence",
    "اسم الجامعة": "university_name",
    "موظف جديد": "is_new_employee",
    "ملاحظات": "notes",
}


def norm_header(h):
    return normalize_arabic(str(h or ""))


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Import employees from Excel into SQLite staging table.")
    parser.add_argument("--excel", required=True, help="Path to Excel file")
    parser.add_argument("--sheet", default=None, help="Sheet name")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    wb = load_workbook(args.excel, data_only=True)
    ws = wb[args.sheet] if args.sheet else wb.active

    headers = [cell.value for cell in ws[1]]
    mapped = {}
    for idx, h in enumerate(headers):
        key = HEADER_MAP.get(norm_header(h)) or HEADER_MAP.get(str(h or "").strip())
        if key:
            mapped[idx + 1] = key

    if not mapped:
        raise SystemExit("No recognized headers found.")

    conn = get_connection()
    resolver = SubjectResolver(conn)
    cur = conn.cursor()

    count = 0

    for row_idx, row in enumerate(ws.iter_rows(min_row=2, values_only=False), start=2):
        record = {}
        for col_idx, key in mapped.items():
            value = row[col_idx - 1].value
            record[key] = value

        if not any(record.values()):
            continue

        national_id = str(record.get("national_id") or "").strip() or None
        ministerial_number = str(record.get("ministerial_number") or "").strip() or None

        if not national_id and not ministerial_number:
            continue

        resolution = resolver.resolve(record.get("specialization_raw"))

        cur.execute("""
            INSERT INTO excel_employee_import(
                source_file, sheet_name, row_number,
                national_id, ministerial_number, full_name,
                school_name, school_national_code,
                specialization_raw, resolved_specialization_id, specialization_source,
                mobile_phone_raw, normalized_mobile_phone,
                years_experience, grade, residence, university_name,
                is_new_employee, notes, raw_json, imported_at
            )
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
        """, (
            str(args.excel),
            ws.title,
            row_idx,
            national_id,
            ministerial_number,
            record.get("full_name"),
            record.get("school_name"),
            record.get("school_national_code"),
            record.get("specialization_raw"),
            resolution.subject_id,
            resolution.source,
            record.get("mobile_phone_raw"),
            normalize_jordan_mobile(record.get("mobile_phone_raw") or ""),
            record.get("years_experience"),
            record.get("grade"),
            record.get("residence"),
            record.get("university_name"),
            1 if str(record.get("is_new_employee") or "").strip().lower() in {"1", "yes", "نعم"} else 0,
            record.get("notes"),
            json.dumps({k: str(v) if v is not None else None for k, v in record.items()}, ensure_ascii=False),
            datetime.now().isoformat()
        ))

        count += 1

    conn.commit()
    conn.close()
    print("Imported rows:", count)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
''')

# build exe 32-bit
# =========================================================
add("scripts/build_exe_32bit.bat", r'''@echo off
setlocal

echo ============================================
echo Build ExamMonitoring2026 as 32-bit EXE
echo Must run with Python 32-bit / x86
echo ============================================

python -c "import struct,sys; bits=struct.calcsize('P')*8; print('Python architecture:', bits, 'bit'); sys.exit(0 if bits==32 else 1)"
if errorlevel 1 (
    echo.
    echo ERROR: You are not using 32-bit Python.
    echo Install Python 3.12 Windows installer 32-bit/x86 and retry.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

echo.
echo Installing dependencies...
pip install -r requirements.txt
pip install pyinstaller

echo.
echo Building EXE...
pyinstaller ^
  --onefile ^
  --windowed ^
  --name ExamMonitoring2026 ^
  --add-data "config;config" ^
  --add-data "src/db/schema.sql;src/db" ^
  src/main.py

if errorlevel 1 (
    echo.
    echo ERROR: Build failed.
    pause
    exit /b 1
)

if exist exam2026.db (
    copy /Y exam2026.db dist\exam2026.db >nul
)

echo.
echo ============================================
echo Build complete.
echo Output: dist\ExamMonitoring2026.exe
echo ============================================
pause
''')

add("tests/test_phone_validation.py", r'''import unittest
from src.core.phone_utils import normalize_jordan_mobile, is_valid_jordan_mobile, validate_phone_field


class TestPhoneValidation(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_valid_077(self):
        self.assertTrue(is_valid_jordan_mobile("0771234567"))

    def test_valid_078(self):
        self.assertTrue(is_valid_jordan_mobile("0781234567"))

    def test_valid_079(self):
        self.assertTrue(is_valid_jordan_mobile("0791234567"))

    def test_valid_076(self):
        self.assertTrue(is_valid_jordan_mobile("0761234567"))

    def test_invalid_075(self):
        self.assertFalse(is_valid_jordan_mobile("0751234567"))

    def test_invalid_short(self):
        self.assertFalse(is_valid_jordan_mobile("077123456"))

    def test_normalize_spaces(self):
        self.assertEqual(normalize_jordan_mobile("077 123 4567"), "0771234567")

    def test_normalize_plus962(self):
        self.assertEqual(normalize_jordan_mobile("+962 77 123 4567"), "0771234567")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
''')

add("tests/test_login.py", r'''import unittest
from src.core.validators import detect_login_type


class TestLoginValidation(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_ministerial_6_digits(self):
        self.assertEqual(detect_login_type("172571"), "MINISTERIAL")

    def test_national_10_digits(self):
        self.assertEqual(detect_login_type("9881018940"), "NATIONAL")

    def test_name_rejected(self):
        self.assertIsNone(detect_login_type("أحمد"))

    def test_5_digits_rejected(self):
        self.assertIsNone(detect_login_type("12345"))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
''')

# =========================================================
# write and zip
# =========================================================
def write_project():
    if ROOT.exists():
        print("Removing existing folder:", ROOT)
        shutil.rmtree(ROOT)

    for rel_path, content in FILES.items():
        file_path = ROOT / rel_path
        file_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        file_path.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")

    print("Project folder created:", ROOT)


def make_zip():
    if ZIP_NAME.exists():
        ZIP_NAME.unlink()

    with zipfile.ZipFile(ZIP_NAME, "w", zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
        for path in ROOT.rglob("*"):
            if path.is_file():
                zf.write(path, path.relative_to(ROOT.parent))

    digest = hashlib.sha256(ZIP_NAME.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    SHA_NAME.write_text("{0}  {1}\n".format(digest, ZIP_NAME.name), encoding="utf-8")

    print("ZIP created:", ZIP_NAME)
    print("SHA-256:", digest)
    print("SHA file:", SHA_NAME)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    write_project()
    make_zip()
```

---

## 3) بعد تشغيل السكربت

ستحصل على مجلد:

```text
ExamMonitoring2026_32bit
```

وداخله المشروع كاملًا.

شغّل:

```bash
cd ExamMonitoring2026_32bit
python scripts/init_db.py
python src/main.py
```

بيانات الدخول التجريبية:

```text
الرقم الوزاري: 172571
الرقم الوطني: 9881018940
```

بعد تحديد المدرسة، جرّب موظفًا تجريبيًا:

```text
الرقم الوطني: 1234567890
الرقم الوزاري: 654321
```

---

## 4) لبناء EXE 32-bit

يجب أن يكون Python المستخدم **32-bit / x86**.

من داخل مجلد المشروع:

```bash
scripts\build_exe_32bit.bat
```

الناتج:

```text
dist\ExamMonitoring2026.exe
```

## 5) ملاحظة مهمة

هذا السكربت ينشئ نسخة تشغيلية أساسية قوية، لكن بعض الشاشات الإدارية الكاملة مثل:

- إدارة المستخدمين بالواجهة الكاملة.
- إدارة الأذونات بالواجهة الكاملة.
- سجل التدقيق بالواجهة الكاملة.
- توليد PDF رسمي داخلي بدون متصفح.

يمكن إضافتها في مرحلة تالية داخل نفس البنية، لأن المشروع الآن جاهز للتوسع.

### USER
سكربت لانشاء كامل الملفات من ا الى ي

### ASSISTANT
نعم. هذا سكربت Python واحد ينشئ **المشروع كاملًا من الألف إلى الياء**: الواجهات، قاعدة البيانات، الخدمات، التخصصات، Excel، PDF/HTML، الاختبارات، والتوثيق.

احفظه باسم:

```text
create_full_project_a_to_z.py
```

ثم شغّله:

```bash
python create_full_project_a_to_z.py
```

سيُنشئ لك:

```text
ExamMonitoring2026_A_to_Z/
ExamMonitoring2026_A_to_Z.zip
ExamMonitoring2026_A_to_Z.zip.sha256
```

بعدها ادخل المجلد وشغّل:

```bash
cd ExamMonitoring2026_A_to_Z
python scripts/init_db.py
python src/main.py
```

بيانات الدخول التجريبية:

```text
الرقم الوزاري: 172571
الرقم الوطني: 9881018940
```

موظف تجريبي بعد اختيار المدرسة:

```text
الرقم الوطني: 1234567890
الرقم الوزاري: 654321
```
